In half of the transformer blocks, main attention keeps a key-value cache of past observations; in the other half, it is restricted to the current chunk and complemented by a recurrent linear-attention memory whose reads and writes are conditioned on projective camera geometry, so viewpoint enters both memory addressing and stored content. Recurrent readouts flow into subsequent cache-backed blocks and supply their queries with accumulated scene context. On the public MIND memory benchmark and on held-out recorded trajectories, LOCI reproduces revisited content more faithfully than representative world models and a same-recipe full-softmax model; with full history, it lowers peak memory at equal length by about 30% relative to full softmax. With a bounded bank of retained observations, it streams long videos at constant memory and remains more faithful than full softmax under the same budget.

</details>


### [176] [EviRover: Reinforcing Agentic Perception Beyond a Glance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.40230)
*Kaixuan Fan,Kaituo Feng,Tianshuo Peng,Yilei Jiang,Manyuan Zhang,Junke Wang,Xiangyu Yue*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出EviRover，首个通过交互解决视觉感知查询的感知智能体。针对传统单次视觉感知在细粒度细节或知识密集型任务中失败的问题，将其建模为智能体过程。构建了EviRover-SFT-5K和EviRover-RL-12K训练数据，以及EviLens人工验证基准测试（688个实例）。4B参数EviRover在EviLens上平均超越基线30分，性能比肩先进专有模型，并可迁移到WebEyes等其他基准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统视觉感知假设单次图像观察即可完成预测，但在现实场景中常因需要细粒度视觉细节或知识密集型最新信息而失败。现有方法缺乏明确处理"证据不足感知"(perception under insufficient evidence)的能力，需要突破单次观察范式。

Method: 设计两个专用数据生成管道构建训练数据；采用监督微调(SFT)结合智能体强化学习(RL)的方法训练EviRover模型，使其能够通过交互获取图像以外的信息来解决感知查询。

Result: 4B参数EviRover在EviLens基准上平均超越基线模型30分，性能达到与先进专有模型相当水平。迁移测试显示在WebEyes、传统感知基准和通用多模态基准上均有提升，其中BrowseComp-VL提升15分。

Conclusion: 首次成功训练出明确通过交互解决感知查询的智能体，验证了将视觉感知建模为智能体过程的可行性，为感知系统从被动预测向主动信息获取的范式转变提供了有效方案。

Abstract: Visual perception is conventionally formulated as a one-shot prediction from a single glance at the image, under the assumption that the image content and the model's parametric knowledge suffice to resolve the query. This assumption often fails in real-world scenarios that hinge on fine-grained visual details or require knowledge-intensive and up-to-date information. We term such cases \textit{{perception under insufficient evidence}} and formulate perception as an agentic process that can obtain information beyond a single glance. To address the absence of data for this setting, we design two dedicated data generation pipelines, yielding EviRover-SFT-5K and EviRover-RL-12K for training. We further construct EviLens, a human-verified benchmark comprising 688 instances across five perception categories. Building on these data, we present EviRover, to our knowledge the first perception agent explicitly trained to resolve perceptual queries through interaction, using supervised fine-tuning followed by agentic reinforcement learning. Experiments show that the 4B EviRover outperforms its backbone by 30 points on average on EviLens, reaching performance comparable to advanced proprietary models. The gains transfer beyond EviLens to WebEyes, conventional perception benchmarks, and general multimodal benchmarks, including a 15-point improvement on BrowseComp-VL. All code, models, and data are released.

</details>


### [177] [StreamRig: Exploiting Intra-Rig Geometry for Streaming Multi-Camera Odometry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.40244)
*Yufei Wei,Shuhao Ye,Qi Wang,Xin Zheng,Qing Huang,Rong Xiong,Yue Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: StreamRig是一个冻结-流式框架，利用冻结的多视角3D基础模型为校准的多相机系统构建因果流式里程计。通过Rig-Resampler压缩特征、CausalBridge应用因果注意力、 lightweight head回归姿态，仅训练74.6M参数，在四个数据集上实现了比单目流式和离线模型更低的漂移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 移动机器人和车辆携带同步多相机，但许多流式3D基础模型为单目输入设计，未能有效利用相机几何结构，导致校准多相机系统的效率使用成为挑战。

Method: 提出冻结-流式框架StreamRig：冻结多视角3D基础模型作为前端联合感知同步视图；Rig-Resampler压缩特征；CausalBridge应用因果注意力与key-value缓存；lightweight head回归姿态；周期性重锚协议支持长序列稳定姿态估计。两阶段训练：组重定位预训练+因果相机训练。

Result: 在NCLT、TartanGround、KITTI-360和自收集数据集ZJH上评估，训练仅用模拟数据，零样本泛化到真实场景。在所有四个数据集上实现比单目流式和离线模型更低的平移和旋转漂移，同时保持低推理成本。

Conclusion: StreamRig成功将冻结的多视角3D基础模型应用于流式里程计，仅训练少量参数即可在零样本场景下取得优异性能，验证了冻结-流式框架在多相机系统上的有效性。

Abstract: Mobile robots and vehicles carry synchronized multi-camera rigs, yet many streaming 3D foundation models are designed for monocular input, leaving efficient use of rig geometry a challenge. We present StreamRig, a freeze-and-stream framework that builds causal streaming odometry for calibrated rigs on a frozen multi-view 3D foundation model. The frozen front-end jointly perceives the synchronized views using rig calibration. A Rig-Resampler compresses their features, a CausalBridge applies causal attention with a key-value cache, and a lightweight head regresses rig poses. A periodic re-anchoring protocol supports stable pose estimation over long sequences. Only these modules are trained, 74.6M parameters in total, with relative poses as the sole supervision. Our two-stage training strategy combines group relocalization pretraining with causal rig training to transfer the geometric priors of the frozen front-end and the alignment ability of the pretrained modules to streaming odometry. We evaluate on NCLT, TartanGround, KITTI-360, and our self-collected humanoid-robot dataset ZJH, where training uses only simulation and real-world evaluation is zero-shot. Across all four datasets, StreamRig achieves lower translation and rotation drift than the evaluated non-oracle monocular streaming and rig-aware offline models, while maintaining low inference cost. Ablations and controlled camera-count experiments identify the sources of these gains. We further examine how longer training windows affect inference over longer horizons. Code has been released at https://github.com/WeiYuFei0217/StreamRig.

</details>


### [178] [ComputerSD: Online Self-Distillation from Real-Time Feedback for Computer-Use Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.40253)
*Yong Du,Tongbo Chen,Zhengxi Lu,Yizhou Liu,Bofan Chen,Tao Jiang,Wenhao Xu,Yongliang Shen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: ComputerSD是一种用于计算机使用代理(CUA)的在线自蒸馏方法,通过将已执行GUI转换的实时反馈转换为策略学习指导,结合token级OSPD和轨迹级GRPO进行联合优化,在OSWorld-Verified基准上显著优于仅使用结果奖励的GRPO方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有CUA在线训练主要依赖稀疏的结果奖励,无法为中间动作提供监督信号。虽然on-policy self-distillation (OPSD)能通过特权重评分提供token级学习信号,但直接应用于CUA在线训练面临两个挑战:固定指导可能与学生当前状态不对齐,指导引起的概率变化可能与步骤级正确性冲突。

Method: ComputerSD将已执行GUI转换的实时反馈转换为策略学习指导。微调的GUI分析器在每个动作后产生指导和步骤级价值分数:指导提供特权上下文,分数调节产生的OSPD信号。在完全异步训练框架中联合优化token级OSPD和轨迹级GRPO。

Result: 在OSWorld-Verified上,ComputerSD在通用Qwen3-VL-8B-Thinking和专用EvoCUA-8B backbone上分别比仅使用结果的GRPO高出1.9和4.1个百分点。分布外设置评估进一步支持了ComputerSD的泛化能力。

Conclusion: 通过在线自蒸馏从实时反馈中学习的有效性得到验证,为CUA的训练提供了新的思路,能够更好地利用中间动作信息提升代理性能。

Abstract: Online training enables computer-use agents (CUAs) to improve through interaction with executable environments. However, existing methods primarily rely on sparse outcome rewards, which provide no supervision for intermediate actions. On-policy self-distillation (OPSD) offers token-level learning signals through privileged rescoring, but directly applying it to CUA online training presents two challenges: fixed guidance may become misaligned with the student's current state, and guidance-induced probability shifts may conflict with step-level correctness. We introduce ComputerSD, an online self-distillation method for CUAs that converts real-time feedback from executed GUI transitions into guidance for policy learning. A fine-tuned GUI analyzer produces guidance and a step-level value score after each action; the guidance provides privileged context, while the score regulates the resulting OPSD signals. ComputerSD jointly optimizes token-level OPSD and trajectory-level GRPO in a fully asynchronous training framework. On OSWorld-Verified, ComputerSD outperforms outcome-only GRPO by 1.9 and 4.1 percentage points on the general-purpose Qwen3-VL-8B-Thinking and specialized EvoCUA-8B backbones, respectively. Evaluation in out-of-distribution settings further supports the generalizability of ComputerSD. These results demonstrate the effectiveness of learning from real-time feedback through online self-distillation for CUAs.

</details>


### [179] [Looped Diffusion Transformer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.40305)
*Yong Xien Chng,Tianyi Chen,Wenwen Tong,Haiwen Diao,Zhongang Cai,Lei Yang,Ziwei Liu,Lewei Lu,Dahua Lin,Gao Huang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出Looped-DiT，通过在去噪步骤内循环运行共享Transformer块来增加计算深度，而非增加模型参数数量或去噪步数。核心创新是结合深度监督和自调制注意力来稳定循环特征更新。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统文本到图像模型改进依赖增大模型尺寸或增加去噪步数，作者探索通过在同一去噪步骤内循环运行共享Transformer块来扩展计算，保持参数数量不变的同时增加计算深度。

Method: 提出Looped-DiT方法，包含两个关键组件：1) 深度监督跨中间循环提供监督信号；2) 自调制注意力调节注意力更新，防止局部信息侵蚀。在匹配参数和匹配计算设置下进行实验。

Result: 260M参数的循环模型可超越比其大6.5倍的模型，同时推理计算降低4.9倍。循环深度增加比增加去噪步数获得更大收益，且更深循环可逐步纠正早期错误，表现出潜在推理行为。

Conclusion: 循环计算为扩展视觉生成模型提供了一种有前景的新方式，能够在不增加参数的情况下有效提升模型性能。

Abstract: Improving text-to-image models has traditionally relied on increasing model size or the number of denoising steps. In this work, we explore an alternative way to scale computation by repeatedly running shared Transformer blocks within each denoising step, effectively increasing computational depth while keeping the parameter count fixed. This looped computation enables iterative refinement of internal representations without explicit reasoning tokens. However, naive looping fails to consistently improve image quality. We trace this problem to weak supervision across intermediate loops and unregulated attention updates that progressively erode local information. To overcome these challenges, we propose Looped Diffusion Transformer (Looped-DiT), which combines deep supervision across intermediate loops with self-modulating attention to stabilize looped feature updates. Under matched-parameter and matched-compute settings, Looped-DiT consistently outperforms non-looped baselines. Notably, a 260M-parameter looped model can surpass a model 6.5x larger across multiple text-to-image benchmarks while requiring 4.9x lower inference compute. Beyond this performance gain, we find that looped computation can offer a more effective form of iterative computation for diffusion models, with increasing loop depth yielding larger gains than adding more denoising steps under a fixed inference budget. Furthermore, deeper loops can progressively correct mistakes made in earlier loops, exhibiting behaviors suggestive of latent reasoning. Together, these results show that looped computation offers a promising way to scale visual generation models.

</details>


### [180] [GLARE: Generating Listening Heads with Appropriate Reactions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.40317)
*Zikai Liao,Yumin Suh,Yi Ouyang,Yi-Lun Lee,Yi-Hsuan Tsai,Zhaozheng Yin*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文针对对话中自然倾听行为生成进行研究，提出三方面贡献：1）构建了包含约147小时说话者-倾听者配对视频和64,557个事件级反应标注的数据集；2）提出基于流匹配变换器的音频驱动模型GLARE，采用Qwen2-Audio的韵律条件编码和时序反应损失；3）提出反应导向的评估协议（R-F1、R-tIoU、R-ATD、R-FID）。实验表明该方法在视觉保真度和反应级指标上均优于先前方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前说话头部生成技术快速发展，但生成自然倾听行为（何时反应、如何反应、反应类型）仍探索不足。现有双人对话数据集缺少细粒度的倾听者反应标注，且现有评估指标继承自说话头部和视频生成任务，仅衡量视觉真实性而非反应恰当性。

Method: 1）数据集构建：从RealTalk和 Seamless Interaction中筛选构建倾听头部数据集，包含147小时配对视频和64,557个六类反应事件标注（点头、摇头、微笑、大笑、皱眉、惊讶）。2）模型提出：GLARE音频驱动基线，基于流匹配变换器，采用Qwen2-Audio提取韵律特征作为条件，设计时序反应损失显式监督逐帧反应。3）评估协议：反应导向评估协议，衡量反应发生率（R-F1）、时序对齐（R-tIoU）、非对称时序偏差（R-ATD）和反应区域视觉质量（R-FID）。

Result: 实验结果表明，GLARE在视觉保真度和反应级指标上均优于先前倾听头部方法，验证了反应感知数据、建模和评估对自然倾听行为生成的重要性。

Conclusion: 反应感知的的数据、建模和评估对自然倾听行为生成至关重要。本研究填补了倾听行为生成中数据、模型和评估三方面的空白，为该领域后续研究提供了重要基础。

Abstract: While talking head generation has advanced rapidly, generating natural listener behavior in dyadic conversations, which know when to react, how to react, and with what type of response, remains underexplored. Existing dyadic datasets lack fine-grained listener reaction annotations, and prevailing evaluation metrics inherited from talking-head and video generation measure visual realism rather than whether a listener reacted appropriately. We address these gaps along three aspects. First, we curate a listening-head-specific dataset built from RealTalk and Seamless Interaction, comprising approximately 147 hours of paired speaker-listener videos with 64,557 event-level reaction annotations across six categories: nodding, head shaking, smiling, laughing, frowning, and surprised. Second, we introduce an audio-driven baseline built on a flow-matching transformer, namely GLARE, with prosody conditioning derived from Qwen2-Audio and a temporal reaction loss that explicitly supervises frame-wise reactions. Third, we propose a reaction-oriented evaluation protocol that jointly measures reaction occurrence (R-F1), temporal alignment (R-tIoU), asymmetric temporal deviation (R-ATD), and reaction-region visual quality (R-FID), giving a more behaviorally grounded assessment than visual-quality-only metrics. Experiment results show consistent gains over prior listening-head methods in both visual fidelity and reaction-level metrics, suggesting that reaction-aware data, modeling, and evaluation are critical for natural listening behavior.

</details>


### [181] [MatLoom: Layered Text-to-Material Generation in a Compact Program Space](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.40322)
*Anson Y. Lam,Shuqing Li,Michael R. Lyu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: MatLoom是一种基于预训练语言模型的文本到材质生成系统，通过紧凑的层级化语言生成材质，每个程序由alpha遮罩层组成，可解释为材质贴图。系统使用解析器引导修复和基于预览的批评来修订设计，并搜索噪声种子。在141个提示词的基准测试中，最佳配置在四个平面布局提示对齐指标上优于三个扩散基线模型。其初始程序在平均BLIPScore上已超过所有三个基线模型。保留的程序在跨骨干网络时中位长度为21行。在30名参与者对20个提示词的盲测中，渲染结果获得59.2%的选择，而最受青睐的基线模型仅获得19.3%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 材质生成不仅应产生外观，还应生成构建它的规则。现有的生成方法缺乏对材质构建过程的显式表示，难以进行后续编辑和修改。需要一种方法能够在生成材质的同时保留其构建过程作为资产的一部分。

Method: 提出MatLoom，一种紧凑的层级化语言，用于文本到材质生成。每个程序由alpha遮罩层组成，共享空间表达式定义覆盖和基于物理渲染(PBR)通道。系统包含独立解释器将程序评估为材质贴图，源代码保留命名字段和层参数以便后续编辑。无需任务特定微调，使用解析器引导修复和基于预览的批评来修订材质设计，然后搜索噪声种子同时保持每个候选的剩余源固定。

Result: 在141个提示词和六个骨干网络的基准测试中，最佳配置在所有四个平面布局提示对齐指标上优于三个扩散基线模型。其初始程序已在平均BLIPScore上超过所有三个基线模型。保留的程序跨骨干网络中位长度为21行。盲测中(30名参与者，20个提示词)，渲染获得59.2%的选择，最受欢迎基线为19.3%。

Conclusion: 紧凑可执行程序提供了一种生成提示对齐材质的方法，同时将材质构建作为资产的一部分保留下来。这种方法在提示对齐和人类偏好方面优于扩散基线模型。

Abstract: Material generation should produce not only an appearance, but also the rules that construct it. We introduce MatLoom, a compact, layer-oriented language for text-to-material generation with pretrained language models. Each program composes alpha-masked layers whose shared spatial expressions define coverage and physically based rendering (PBR) channels, making dependencies between patterns, color, and relief explicit. A standalone interpreter evaluates the program into material maps, while the source retains named fields and layer parameters for subsequent authoring. Without task-specific fine-tuning, our pipeline uses parser-guided repair and preview-based critique to revise material designs, then searches noise seeds while keeping each candidate's remaining source fixed. On a curated benchmark of 141 prompts evaluated with six backbones, our best-performing configuration achieves higher mean scores than three diffusion baselines on all four flat-layout prompt-alignment metrics. Its initial programs already exceed all three baselines on mean BLIPScore, before critique or seed search. Retained programs have a median length of 21 lines when pooled across backbones. In a blind four-way comparison involving 30 participants and 20 prompts, our renders receive 59.2% of choices, compared with 19.3% for the most-preferred baseline. Compact executable programs thus offer a way to generate prompt-aligned materials while retaining their construction as part of the asset.

</details>


### [182] [Atomizer-IO: Beyond Pixels, Patches and Grids](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.40320)
*Hugo Riffaud de Turckheim,Sylvain Lobry,Nicolas Houdré,Damien Robert,Roberto Interdonato,Diego Marcos*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Atomizer-IO是一种新型视觉架构，它将观测置于首位，从物理关系中推导结构。该方法使用原子表示法，每个观测由测量和获取元数据描述，通过局部交叉注意力将观测映射到可任意放置的锚点。该架构可应用于非规则栅格输入的传感数据，如3D点云，并在多个任务上与专用EO架构竞争，同时提供推理成本的后训练控制和良好的计算-性能权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前视觉架构假设观测位于规则栅格上，这对自然图像有效，但对通道、时间采样、空间分辨率和几何形状可变的传感数据具有限制性。通用的基于集合的架构虽然移除了栅格，但也丢失了有用的空间归纳偏置。

Method: 提出Atomizer-IO架构：1)基于数据的原子表示，每个观测由其测量和获取元数据描述；2)局部交叉注意力将观测映射到可任意放置的锚点。该设计逐步放松栅格假设，从变化的输入栅格配置和不完整通道集，到灵活输出密度，最终实现无栅格输入。

Result: Atomizer-IO在大多数任务上与灵活的EO专用架构竞争，同时提供推理成本的后训练控制和竞争力的计算-性能权衡。相同 formulation无需架构重新设计即可扩展到无序3D点 cloud，表明原子接口可超越规则栅格输入进行泛化。

Conclusion: 像素、patch和栅格不需要定义传感架构的接口，原子表示方法为处理多样化传感数据提供了更灵活的通用框架。

Abstract: Most vision architectures assume that observations lie on a regular grid, an effective abstraction for natural images but a restrictive one for sensing data whose channels, temporal sampling, spatial resolution, and geometry can vary. Generic set-based architectures remove the grid, but also remove useful spatial inductive biases. We introduce Atomizer-IO, an architecture that places observations first and derives structure from their physical relationships. Building on top of an atomic representation of the data, each observation is described by its measurement and acquisition metadata, while local cross-attention maps observations to anchor points that can be arbitrarily placed. We evaluate this design by progressively relaxing the grid assumption, from varying input raster configurations and incomplete channel sets to flexible output density and, ultimately, inputs without a raster grid. Atomizer-IO is competitive with flexible EO-specific architectures on most tasks, while offering post-training control over inference cost and competitive compute--performance trade-offs. The same formulation extends without architectural redesign to unordered 3D point clouds, showing that the atomic interface generalizes beyond regular raster inputs. These results suggest that pixels, patches, and grids do not need to define the interface of a sensing architecture.

</details>


### [183] [ViTeX-Bench: Benchmarking High-Fidelity Video Scene Text Editing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.40356)
*Xinghao Chen,Xiangbo Gao,Jiongze Yu,Yuheng Wu,Zhengzhong Tu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: ViTeX-Bench是一个视频场景文本编辑基准，包含387个真实视频数据集和13个指标的三轴评估协议。提出的ViTeX-Edit-14B在文本正确性指标上表现最优，但准确文本、时间稳定性和场景保真度之间存在固有权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视频编辑领域虽有进展，但精确局部编辑仍不成熟。场景文本编辑在图像领域研究充分，但视频场景文本编辑的高视觉质量、时间一致性和编辑局部性仍探索不足。现有资源缺乏成对真实视频数据，传统视频编辑指标无法直接衡量文本随时间的正确性。

Method: 提出ViTeX-Bench基准套件，包含ViTeX-Dataset数据集（387个720p真实视频，230个训练样本，157个评估样本）和三轴评估协议（13个指标，每轴一个主要指标+帕累托比较）。还提出ViTeX-Edit-14B参考编辑器，采用运动对齐的字形-视频条件微调。

Result: 在8个来自4个编辑家族的基线模型中，ViTeX-Edit-14B达到CharAcc 0.688，为评估的视频原生编辑器中最高均值；在原始编辑器输出中具有最低的文本裁剪Warp值。但准确文本、时间稳定性和场景保真度三者仍难以同时实现。

Conclusion: ViTeX-Bench为视频场景文本编辑提供了可复现的研究基础。ViTeX-Edit-14B在视频原生编辑器中达到最高CharAcc 0.688，但准确文本、时间稳定性和场景保真度三者仍难以同时实现。评估表明在文本正确性、视觉/时间质量、编辑局部性三个维度存在显著权衡。

Abstract: Recent video generation is increasingly realistic and controllable, yet video editing remains less developed, particularly for precise local edits that must preserve the original scene dynamics. Video scene text editing replaces text on scene surfaces, such as storefront signs, whiteboards, and product labels, while preserving the surrounding content, motion, and camera dynamics. Although scene text editing is well studied for images, video scene text editing that achieves high visual quality, temporal consistency, and edit locality remains underexplored. Existing resources offer limited paired real-video data, and general video-editing metrics do not directly measure whether the requested text remains correct over time. We introduce ViTeX-Bench, a benchmark suite comprising ViTeX-Dataset and a three-axis evaluation protocol. The dataset contains 387 real-world 720p videos with text-region masks and editing instructions: 230 provide reviewed, pipeline-generated paired edits for training, and 157 form a frozen evaluation split. The protocol evaluates text correctness, visual and temporal quality, and edit locality through 13 metrics, with one primary metric per axis and a Pareto comparison of their trade-offs. OCR calibration, human evaluation, and annotation-sensitivity analyses support the interpretation of these scores. Across eight baselines from four editing families, accurate text, temporal stability, and scene preservation remain difficult to achieve together. We also release ViTeX-Edit-14B, an open-source reference editor fine-tuned on the paired training split with motion-aligned glyph-video conditioning. It achieves CharAcc 0.688, the highest mean among the evaluated video-native editors, and the lowest comparable text-crop Warp among raw editor outputs. ViTeX-Bench provides a reproducible foundation for studying these trade-offs in video scene text editing.

</details>


### [184] [I Have a Stream: Making Self-Supervised Learning Work on Continuous Video](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.40333)
*Ivan Martinović,Lukas Knobel,Yuki M. Asano*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文研究自监督学习在连续视频流中的表现,模仿婴儿视觉发育模式,而非传统的独立采样和全局打乱方式。作者构建了WT++(95小时城市步行视频数据集),并提出StreamMAE方法来解决流式预训练中的挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自监督学习的灵感来源于婴儿视觉发育,但标准训练流程与此大不相同:图像独立采样并在epoch间全局打乱。本文研究连续视频流的自监督学习,其中帧按时间顺序消费,使用严格的滑动窗口批次,无全局重排或多epoch重放。

Method: 构建了95小时城市步行视频数据集WT++,结合综合评估套件。提出StreamMAE,保留MAE重建核心目标,同时通过流感知正则化和运动偏向裁剪来适应输入管道。

Result: 对比学习和蒸馏方法在此设置下表现困难,MAE更鲁棒但仍不如标准i.i.d.预训练。主要挑战是批次内高相似度(帧几乎重复)。StreamMAE优于流式基线,与相同视频数据上i.i.d. MAE相当,与ImageNet预训练MAE具有竞争力,并随预训练流从12小时扩展到95小时呈正向扩展。

Conclusion: StreamMAE能有效从连续视频流中学习,通过流感知正则化和运动偏向裁剪解决批次内相似度问题,为流式自监督学习提供了有效方案。

Abstract: Self-supervised learning draws inspiration from infant visual development, yet standard training pipelines bear little resemblance to it: images are independently sampled and globally shuffled across epochs. We study self-supervised learning from continuous video streams, where frames are consumed in temporal order using strict sliding-window batches, without global reshuffling or multi-epoch replay. To this end, we construct WT++, a 95-hour urban walking-tour video dataset for streaming pretraining. Combined with a comprehensive evaluation suite we find that contrastive and distillation-based methods struggle in this setting, while MAE is more robust but still falls short of standard i.i.d. pretraining. We find that high inter-batch similarity, caused by sliding-window consumption across consecutive batches, does not explain this gap. The main challenge is high intra-batch similarity, where frames within each batch are near-duplicates. To mitigate this, we propose StreamMAE, which preserves the core MAE reconstruction objective while adapting the input pipeline with stream-aware regularization and motion-biased crop selection. StreamMAE outperforms streaming baselines, matches i.i.d. MAE trained on the same video data, remains competitive with ImageNet-pretrained MAE, and scales positively as the pretraining stream grows from 12 to 95 hours.

</details>


### [185] [Image Classifiers are Efficient Self-Supervised Video Representation Learners](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.40347)
*Owais Iqbal,Sudipta Sarkar,Shyam Marjit,Omprakash Chakraborty,Anirban Chakraborty,Abir Das*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: VideoMSN是一种用于高效自监督视频时空表示学习的掩码孪生网络框架，将视频表示为超级图像（帧组成的网格），使用预训练的图像ViT编码器进行视频表示学习。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决视频自监督学习中依赖重型3D架构或重建自编码器的问题，实现更高效的视频表示学习。

Method: 将视频表示为超级图像，构造空间patch掩码和时间帧掩码两种视图，使用共享Vision Transformer编码器对齐嵌入，采用无解码器的掩码孪生损失。

Result: 在Kinetics-400、UCF101、HMDB51上实现最先进性能，比之前方法减少32倍和160倍的视频预训练轮次，低样本分类表现优异。

Conclusion: 验证了所学表示在标签稀缺场景中的强迁移能力，证明了图像基础模型可有效迁移到视频领域。

Abstract: We introduce VideoMSN, a Masked Siamese Network framework for efficient self-supervised spatio-temporal representation learning in videos. Instead of relying on heavy 3D architectures or reconstruction-based autoencoders for learning with unlabeled data, we repurpose standard image Vision Transformers by representing videos as super images which are grids composed of frames sampled from videos. From each super image, we construct two views: one with spatial patch masking and the other with temporal frame masking, ensuring no information leakage across frames. A shared Vision Transformer (ViT) encoder aligns their embeddings using a masked Siamese loss, capturing both motion and appearance cues without reconstruction. Our decoder-free formulation leverages an image foundation model towards efficient video representation learning. Starting from pretrained DINO-v3 and DeiT-v3 image encoders, VideoMSN achieves state-of-the-art performance on Kinetics-400, UCF101, and HMDB51 while requiring up to $32\times$ fewer and $160\times$ fewer video pretraining epochs compared to prior video self-supervised learning methods. Our proposed approach also shows strong performance in low-shot classification, confirming the transferability of the learned representations in a label-scarce scenario. Project Page: https://cvir.github.io/projects/videomsn.

</details>


### [186] [AssemblyWorld: Rethinking 3D Assembly with General-Purpose Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.40353)
*Jiahao Zhang,Yeying Fan,Moitreya Chatterjee,Suhas Lohit,Bernhard Egger,Tim K. Marks,Anoop Cherian,Stephen Gould*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文提出了AssemblyWorld，一个交互式3D环境，用于评估预训练通用智能体在无需额外微调的情况下完成3D组装任务的能力，并构建了包含100个组装任务的基准测试AssemblyWorldBench。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究预训练通用智能体是否能在没有专门针对组装任务的微调情况下，通过视觉交互完成3D组装任务。

Method: 构建交互式3D环境，智能体通过2D视图感知零件几何信息而非直接访问网格数据，在有图像或组装手册的情况下操作刚性零件，最终通过几何评估组装结果。

Result: 最强系统达到80.9%的零件准确率，但完整组装成功率仅为59.4%。开源系统明显落后于闭源系统。分析显示智能体在组装过程中会进行修订，但仍存在残余定位误差。

Conclusion: AssemblyWorld提供了评估交互式组装智能体能力的共同设置，揭示了近似结构恢复与精确重建之间存在显著差距。

Abstract: The task of 3D assembly requires translating an understanding of parts and their relationships into precise spatial arrangements. Can pretrained general-purpose agents assemble objects through visual interaction without additional assembly-specific fine-tuning? To investigate this question, we introduce AssemblyWorld, an interactive 3D environment in which agents inspect rendered views and manipulate supplied rigid parts, guided by images or assembly manuals when available. Agents perceive part geometry through 2D views rather than direct access to mesh vertices or faces, while their resulting assemblies are evaluated geometrically. Building on this environment, we construct AssemblyWorldBench, comprising 100 assembly tasks across 80 objects spanning furniture, industrial assembly, and fracture reassembly. Evaluating eight agent systems reveals substantial differences in their capabilities. The strongest system achieves 80.9% part accuracy but 59.4% complete-assembly success. The evaluated open-source systems lag substantially behind their stronger closed-source peers in both execution reliability and assembly accuracy. Analyses of visual references, interaction trajectories, and failures show how agents revise assemblies while leaving residual positioning errors. AssemblyWorld provides a common setting for both assessing the capabilities of interactive assembly agents and characterizing the gap between approximate structure recovery and precise reconstruction.

</details>


### [187] [Physis-Lang: Self-Evolving Language as a Physical Representation for Video World Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.40358)
*Liming Lu,Xianzheng Ma,Wenkun He,Guanqi Zhan,Yilin Zhao,Junyu Chen,Mengyao Xu,Jiaojiao Fan,Wenhang Ge,Yuchao Gu,Yunze Liu,Boyi Li,Zhen Dong,Victor Prisacariu,Ming-Yu Liu,Song Han,Han Cai*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了Physis-Lang框架，挑战了"自然语言不足以表示物理知识"的传统假设，将物理语言作为数据 curation、模型训练和视频生成的共享可优化表示。Physis-Lang从开源Cosmos3-Nano模型起步，在四个物理视频基准上超越了专有Veo 3.1模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视频世界模型常产生视觉上合理但违反基本物理原理的视频。传统方法认为自然语言不足以表示物理知识，因此引入额外的视觉、潜在、数值或规划信号。本文重新审视这一假设，探索仅使用物理语言作为共享表示的可能性。

Method: 1) 使用物理语言描述物理过程的相关实体、原因、交互、支配原理、时间演化和效应；2) 构建PhysCapBench基准，将物理过程分解为原子断言，使用召回率和精确率评估 caption；3) 使用 agentic 循环分析断言级错误并优化物理 caption 指令；4) 将模型缺陷转化为文本描述，使用语言引导检索覆盖缺失物理过程的视觉多样化视频。

Result: 在四个广泛使用的物理视频基准上，使用 Wan 和 Cosmos 骨干网络均取得一致的物理合理性提升。特别地，从开源 Cosmos3-Nano 骨干网络起步，Physis-Lang 增强的模型超越了领先的专有 Veo 3.1 模型。

Conclusion: 物理语言可以作为有效的共享表示，用于提高视频生成中的物理合理性。通过将物理知识编码为语言并结合迭代优化和语言引导检索，可以突破传统方法对额外视觉或数值信号的依赖。

Abstract: Video world models are expected to predict how the physical world evolves, yet they often produce visually plausible videos that violate basic physical principles. Existing approaches commonly assume that natural language is insufficient to represent the physical knowledge required for reliable generation, and therefore introduce additional visual, latent, numerical, or planning-based signals. We revisit this assumption and introduce Physis-Lang, a self-evolving framework that treats physical language as a shared and optimizable representation across data curation, model training, and video generation. Physis-Lang represents physical processes through language that describes their relevant entities, causes, interactions, governing principles, temporal evolution, and effects. To improve this representation, we construct PhysCapBench, which decomposes physical processes into atomic assertions and evaluates captions using recall and precision. An agentic loop iteratively analyzes assertion-level errors and refines the instruction used to produce physical captions. Physis-Lang further converts model deficiencies into textual descriptions and uses language-guided retrieval to identify visually diverse videos that cover missing physical processes. Experiments on four widely used physical video benchmarks with Wan and Cosmos backbones demonstrate consistent improvements in physical plausibility. Notably, starting from open-source Cosmos3-Nano backbones, our Physis-Lang-enhanced models surpass the leading proprietary Veo 3.1 model.

</details>


### [188] [Multimodal Flow: Unified Flow Modeling of Language and Vision in Embedding Spaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.40362)
*Hongyuan Tao,Xinggang Wang,Lianghui Zhu,Yongkang Li,Yunchao Wei,Bin Feng,Shaoyu Chen,Qian Zhang,Chang Huang,Kai Yu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Multimodal Flow是一种统一的连续生成模型，通过共享的chunk-causal flow backbone和联合注意力机制，将语言和视觉建模为连续超块表示，避免了视觉量化瓶颈和模态相关目标的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的统一多模态模型要么将语言和量化图像建模为离散token，要么结合离散语言预测与连续图像生成。前者引入视觉量化瓶颈，后者需要模态相关目标函数和采样程序。完全连续建模可以避免这些权衡，但目前在多模态预训练中探索不足。

Method: 提出统一连续架构Multimodal Flow：1)将文本块和图像组织为有序连续超块，保留文本token顺序和视觉空间结构；2)通过Flow Matching在超块上学习单一向量场；3)联合注意力实现跨模态交互，模态特定前馈网络处理各模态；4)训练时并行预测多个目标块，推理时顺序生成超块。模型在0.6B、1.2B、1.6B规模下进行预训练。

Result: 仅使用150B预训练token，MF-1在GenEval和DPG-Bench上平均得分82.8，在VQAv2、MMBench和POPE上得分75.3，与使用更多数据训练的统一模型相当。在匹配数据、优化和参数预算下，Multimodal Flow优于代表性的混合和离散模型。

Conclusion: 连续基于块的嵌入流建模是统一多模态建模的一种新的完全连续范式，为多模态预训练提供了新的研究方向。

Abstract: We present Multimodal Flow, a fully continuous generative model of language and vision. Most unified multimodal models either model both language and quantized images as discrete tokens or combine discrete language prediction with continuous image generation. The former introduces a visual quantization bottleneck. The latter requires modality-dependent objectives and sampling procedures. Fully continuous modeling avoids these trade-offs and enables a shared generative process, but remains underexplored for multimodal pretraining. Multimodal Flow introduces a unified continuous architecture that integrates multimodal continuous representations with a shared chunk-causal flow backbone. It organizes text blocks and images as ordered continuous hyperchunks, preserving textual token order and visual spatial structure. The backbone learns a single vector field over these hyperchunks through Flow Matching. Joint attention enables cross-modal interaction, while modality-specific feed-forward networks process each modality. The model predicts multiple target chunks in parallel during training and generates hyperchunks sequentially at inference. We instantiate MF-1 and pretrain it on multimodal data. Across 0.6B, 1.2B, and 1.6B scales, continued pretraining consistently improves multimodal modeling. With only 150B pretraining tokens, MF-1 achieves an average score of 82.8 across GenEval and DPG-Bench and 75.3 across VQAv2, MMBench, and POPE, remaining competitive with unified models trained on substantially more data. Under matched data, optimization, and parameter budgets, Multimodal Flow further outperforms representative hybrid and discrete models. These results establish continuous chunk-based embedding flow modeling as a new fully continuous paradigm for unified multimodal modeling. The related code and model are publicly released at https://github.com/hustvl/Multimodal-Flow.

</details>


<div id='cs.MA'></div>

# cs.MA [[Back]](#toc)

### [189] [Absorbing State Phase Transitions in Multi-Agent Search](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38327)
*Wenwen Zheng,Yuzhe Yang,Helen Qu,Xin Eric Wang,Haewon Jeong*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 本文研究基于大语言模型的多智能体系统中，通过吸收态相变理论预测多智能体搜索任务的成功率。研究首先将搜索任务分为四类，从理论推导出临界通信度d_c，然后在软件配置调试和物理机制发现等真实任务上验证，发现理论与实际结果存在差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型多智能体系统能产生复杂动力学行为，但设计最优通信拓扑以完成任务仍是活跃研究问题。作者希望利用统计力学形式主义，特别是吸收态相变理论，来预测多智能体搜索任务的成功。

Method: 1）借鉴组合搜索经典结果，将搜索任务分为四类；2）理论推导临界通信度d_c（每个智能体最少通信邻居数），超过该值错误假设不再失控扩散；3）在软件配置调试和物理机制发现等真实任务上评估前沿LLM多智能体系统。

Result: 理论与实际吻合度有限。LLM智能体可能不与邻居通信，且可能发展出对个体有益但限制协作收益的策略。

Conclusion: 通过吸收态相变理论预测多智能体搜索任务成功是可行方向，但当前LLM智能体行为与理论假设存在偏差，需进一步研究智能体通信和协作机制。

Abstract: Nontrivial dynamics can emerge in large language model (LLM)-based multi-agent systems, and preliminary evidence exists that formalisms from statistical mechanics can be effective at modeling and predicting such behaviors. In parallel, designing multi-agent communication topology for optimal task-solving is an active research question. In this paper, we focus on predicting the success of multi-agent search tasks using the formalism of absorbing state phase transitions. We first taxonomize search tasks into four types, informed by classical results in combinatorial search. We then theoretically derive a critical communication degree $d_c$, the minimum number of agents each agent can communicate with, above which incorrect hypotheses do not proliferate uncontrollably and the search enters the solved state. Finally, we evaluate frontier LLM-based multi-agent systems on real-world search and discovery tasks, software configuration debugging and physical mechanism discovery, and find that agreement with theory is mixed. LLM agents may not communicate with their neighbors and can develop strategies that are individually beneficial but limits the benefits of collaboration.

</details>


### [190] [PANDA: A Decentralized Architecture with Flexible Orchestration for Scalable, Fault-Tolerant Multi-Agent Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38482)
*Matthew D. Laws,Cristina Nita-Rotaru*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: PANDA是一种去中心化的多智能体系统架构，通过解耦集体通信与团队通信、支持智能体同时参与多个团队、负载均衡任务、分离架构与编排策略、支持三种规划执行模式（星型、链型、网型），以及使用信任网络模型实现治理，解决现有LLM多智能体系统无法可靠高效地大规模解决多步骤任务的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM多智能体系统架构无法可靠高效地大规模解决多步骤任务，具体问题包括：难以支持大量智能体和并发任务、缺乏容错能力、无法管理智能体交互、以及无法适应不同任务的多样化规划和执行模式需求。

Method: PANDA采用去中心化架构，核心设计包括：1)解耦集体通信与团队通信；2)支持智能体同时参与多个团队实现负载均衡；3)将底层架构与编排策略分离，支持星型、链型、网型三种模式；4)使用web-of-trust模型进行去中心化治理；5)故障检测与动态重规划恢复机制。

Result: 在HotPotQA基准测试上，PANDA可扩展至数千个智能体，团队组建仅需毫秒级，在保持最先进准确率的同时实现最高8倍效率提升，并在现有系统失败的故障场景下维持100%任务完成率。

Conclusion: PANDA通过去中心化设计成功解决了大规模多智能体系统的可扩展性、效率、容错和治理问题，为LLM多智能体系统在真实场景中的大规模应用提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Existing architectures for LLM-based multi-agent systems (MAS) cannot reliably and efficiently solve multi-step tasks at scale: they struggle to support large numbers of agents and concurrent tasks, tolerate failures, govern agent interactions, and accommodate the diverse planning and execution patterns different tasks require. We present PANDA, a decentralized architecture that connects a large collective of heterogeneous, independently administered agents, letting them discover each other's capabilities and self-organize into small specialized teams per task. PANDA scales by decoupling collective communication from team communication, allowing agents to participate in multiple teams simultaneously, load-balancing tasks across the collective, and scheduling concurrent work within each agent. PANDA further separates the underlying architecture from the orchestration strategy, supporting three planning and execution patterns (star, chain, and mesh) that can be selected according to the structure and requirements of each task. PANDA detects infrastructure and orchestration failures and recovers affected tasks by dynamically replanning around failed components. Finally, to provide governance without a centralized service that would limit scalability, PANDA uses a web-of-trust model to constrain agent interactions to established trust relationships. We evaluate PANDA on the HotPotQA benchmark, demonstrating that it scales to thousands of agents, assembles teams in milliseconds, matches state-of-the-art accuracy at up to 8x the efficiency, and sustains 100% task completion under faults where existing systems fail.

</details>


### [191] [From Solo to Social Learning: Characterizing Recursive Social Improvement in LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38516)
*Kunal Jha,Max Kleiman-Weiner,Natasha Jaques*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 研究当每个智能体追求自身奖励时，自我改进的LLM能否通过相互学习来改善整个群体，称之为递归社会改进。研究发现LLM通过复制同伴可以提高学习效率，但尚未能提高学习效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自改进方法通常优化单一系统，多智能体框架往往让所有模型追求共同目标。作者提出不同问题：当每个智能体追求自身奖励时，自改进的LLM能否相互学习以改善整个群体？

Method: 研究构建了修订技能文件的智能体群体，允许它们决定是否、何时以及向谁复制。独立搜索、向同伴学习和行动共享同一token预算。在受控环境中测试已建立的社会学习算法，并让模型编写和修订自己的技能。

Result: 在受控环境中，传统社会学习算法受益于同伴，但三个LLM智能体表现不如独立学习者——每token获得的奖励更少，探索范围过窄或在行动前耗尽token。让模型编写和修订技能后，观察同伴改变了它们的改进方式——帮助一个模型更快找到有用技能，另一个减少私人搜索支出。但两者都未在相同成本下超越独立学习者。技能被复制、修订和传递，使一个发现可进一步搜索，但这些交换使群体集中在更少的独立发现周围。

Conclusion: LLM可以通过向同伴复制来提高学习效率，但尚未能提高学习效果。复制使探索更加高效，但以牺牲多样性为代价，群体最终集中在更少的独立发现上。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) can now improve themselves by revising the instructions they follow, and LLM agents are increasingly orchestrated to work together on complex problems. However, self-improvement methods typically optimize one system at a time, and multi-agent frameworks often have every model work toward a shared goal. We ask a different question. When each agent pursues its own reward, can self-improving LLMs learn from one another well enough to improve the whole population? We call this capability recursive social improvement. We study populations that revise skill files and choose whether, when, and whom to copy from. Independent search, learning from peers, and acting all share one token budget. In controlled environments, established social-learning algorithms benefit from peers, but three LLMs do not. They earn less reward per token than solo learners, and explore too narrowly or run out of tokens before acting. We then let the models write and revise their own skills. Observing peers changes how they improve, helping one model find useful skills sooner and another spend less on private search. Neither, however, outperforms independent learners at the same cost. Skills are copied, revised, and passed on, so one discovery can seed further search. Yet these exchanges concentrate the population around fewer independent discoveries. Together, these results show that LLMs can make learning more efficient by copying from peers, but not yet more effective.

</details>


### [192] [Recursive Organization Improvement: A Modeling Specification for Human--Agent Organizations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38643)
*Zilong Wang*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个递归组织改进的建模规范，通过可执行检查器、公共记录映射和受控模拟进行评估。研究了证据获取、重复使用和及时更新作为与评估者替换分离的机制。核心发现：在 stationary 环境中，累积证据将平衡评估的每任务归一化净价值从0.45224提高到0.48007；重复重新评估的相对劣势从0.01702降至0.00007。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 更强的AI代理不一定自动产生更好的组织，团队必须学习保留何种工作安排以及何时重新考虑它们。这需要理解组织改进的机制，特别是证据获取、重复使用和及时更新的作用。

Method: 1) 提出连接行为体可见历史、组织记忆、决策权和证据承载变更契约的建模规范；2) 机制研究跨越六种决策规则、三种记忆条件和三种任务环境；3) 在固定资源上限下进行受控模拟

Result: 1) 累积证据提高归一化净价值(0.45224→0.48007)；2) 重置证据时重新评估劣势为0.01702，累积证据时降至0.00007；3) 无限期保留延迟适应，有限窗口可恢复性能；4) 匹配试验获取和标签重用将重新评估收益从0.00607降至0.00191；5) 程序替换在测试的逆转时间无稳定收益

Conclusion: 证据获取、重复使用和及时更新是与评估者替换相分离的机制，在评估组织改进时必须加以区分。这一发现有助于设计更有效的AI驱动组织学习系统。

Abstract: Stronger AI agents do not automatically produce better organizations: teams must also learn which work arrangements to retain and when to reconsider them. We propose a modeling specification for recursive organization improvement and evaluate it through an executable checker, a public-record mapping, and controlled simulation. The specification connects actor-visible histories, organizational memory, decision rights, and evidence-carrying change contracts. The mechanism study crosses six decision rules, three memory conditions, and three task environments under fixed resource ceilings. In a stationary environment, cumulative evidence raises balanced evaluation's normalized net value per task from 0.45224 to 0.48007. Repeated reassessment's disadvantage relative to this comparator falls from 0.01702 with reset evidence to 0.00007 with cumulative evidence. A reversal of the best workflow reveals the opposite cost: indefinite retention delays adaptation, while a finite window restores eventual performance at a transition cost. In exploratory controls, matching trial acquisition and label reuse reduces the apparent reassessment gain from 0.00607 to 0.00191. Program replacement adds no stable benefit across the tested reversal times. The study identifies evidence acquisition, reuse, and timely updating as mechanisms that must be separated from evaluator replacement when assessing organizational improvement.

</details>


### [193] [CollabFlow: Recursive Self-Improvement of Agent Collaboration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38662)
*Xiao Huang,Mingda Zhang,Junming Zhang,Qiang Huang,Hanwen Zhang,Yue Dai,Zijia Wang,Xiaoying Tang*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: CollabFlow是一个递归自改进(RSI)系统，通过可训练的Collab-Director构建智能体团队，使用证据条件通信协议和协作轨迹平衡(CTB)目标，在12个数据集上超越所有基线并持续改进。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM多智能体协作存在三个问题：1)协作由操作员预定义，缺乏自主学习；2)拓扑学习仅复制通信内容，传播错误；3)奖励最大化聚焦于少数团队，忽视多样性。

Method: 提出CollabFlow系统：1)可训练Collab-Director构建智能体团队；2)冻结执行器运行团队；3)每轮结果重新训练director；4)协作图边使用证据条件通信协议（接收方仅在发送方证据更强时才采纳不同答案）；5)跨轮使用协作轨迹平衡(CTB)目标，奖励按比例分配给多个优秀团队。

Result: 在12个数据集上，CollabFlow优于所有基线方法，且能够跨轮持续改进。

Conclusion: CollabFlow成功实现了多智能体协作的递归自改进，通过学习通信协议和团队构建策略，有效提升了系统性能并保持了团队多样性。

Abstract: Recursive self-improvement (RSI) lets a system improve from its own outcomes; in LLM-based multi-agent systems, Agents refine one another within a task, and outcomes improve how they collaborate across tasks. However, existing multi-agent collaboration leaves this loop open: collaboration is pre-defined at the operator level, topology-only learning keeps verbatim exchange that propagates errors, and reward maximization on a system's own outcomes concentrates on a few teams. To address these challenges, we propose CollabFlow, an RSI system of Learned Agent Collaboration: a trainable Collab-Director constructs teams of complete Agents, a frozen executor runs them, and each round's outcomes retrain the director. Within each round, the edges of a collaboration graph carry protocols of Evidence-Conditioned Communication: a receiver adopts a differing answer only when the sender's evidence is stronger by a margin, so the director learns who communicates and how. Across rounds, we further propose Collaborative Trajectory Balance (CTB), a flow-based objective that credits each team once across its construction orders and targets a reward-proportional distribution over teams, so several good teams stay in play. We also bound how far this self-generated target moves between rounds, which shrinks as records accumulate. On twelve datasets, CollabFlow outperforms all baselines and keeps improving across rounds. Code is available at https://anonymous.4open.science/r/CollabFlow-631E.

</details>


### [194] [Where Do Multi-Agent Systems Fail? Evidence-Grounded Diagnosis of Collective Mechanisms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38761)
*Zhengye Han*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 本文研究多智能体系统中集体机制失效但答案正确的情况，提出"诊断契约"方法来确定执行记录是否足以判定某机制被破坏。实验表明：机制破坏常导致正确答案，内部记录比公共输出能检测更多违规，但通用prompt常产生过度自信的误判。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当多智能体系统给出正确答案时，容易假设各智能体按预期共享、检查和使用信息，但实际上系统可能破坏了某个集体机制（信息路由、存储、使用的规则）仍返回正确结果，而错误答案很少能揭示具体哪个机制失效。因此需要研究如何从执行记录判定特定机制是否被破坏。

Method: 提出"诊断契约"方法，将"何为违规"与"哪些执行记录可确立违规"分离，得出违规被支持、排除或未知三种结论。实验通过在机制作用的步骤回放执行：保持原样、破坏机制、恢复机制。对四种机制测试，使用LLM诊断器比较内部记录与公共输出的检测能力，并检验通用prompt与契约prompt的差异。

Result: 1) 机制破坏常导致答案仍正确；2) LLM诊断器从内部记录检测到的违规远多于从公共输出；3) 相同记录下通用prompt常声称确定性但实际记录不支持，契约prompt基本避免此问题；4) 诊断契约可窄化应用于独立开发的系统；5) 在基准上近乎完美的诊断行为移植到独立工作流时显著退化。

Conclusion: 正确答案不能替代对集体机制运作过程的记录；在一个基准上达成一致的诊断器并不代表能迁移到另一个系统。系统设计需保留充分的执行记录，并针对具体系统定制诊断方法。

Abstract: When a multi-agent system answers correctly, it is tempting to conclude that its agents shared, checked, and used information as intended. Yet a system can break one of its collective mechanisms, the rules that govern how agents route, admit, store, and act on shared information, and still return the right answer, while a wrong answer rarely reveals which mechanism failed. We ask what evidence from an execution is sufficient to conclude that a particular mechanism was violated. Our answer is a diagnostic contract, which separates what counts as a violation from which execution records can establish one, and concludes that a violation is supported, ruled out, or unknown; removing records can make this conclusion unknown but never reverse it. We test contracts for four mechanisms by replaying executions from the step where a mechanism acts, once unchanged, once with the mechanism broken, and once with it restored. Broken mechanisms often left the answer correct. An LLM diagnoser detected many more violations from internal records than from public outputs, yet with identical records a generic prompt often claimed certainty the records did not support, which prompts stating the contracts largely avoided. The contracts also applied, in narrow form, to mechanisms in independently developed systems, but a diagnostic behavior that was nearly perfect on our benchmark degraded on an independently developed workflow. A correct outcome is therefore no substitute for records of how collective mechanisms operated, and agreement on one benchmark does not show that a diagnoser transfers to another system.

</details>


### [195] [Fast and Scalable Multi-Agent Distribution Matching via Partitioned Optimal Transport](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38960)
*Kooktae Lee,Ruchika Singh*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于最优传输的多智能体系统终端分布匹配可扩展框架，通过将智能体和目标样本分区到空间对应的块中来解决大规模问题的计算瓶颈。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 全局离散传输在大规模系统中计算成本过高，需要一种可扩展的方法来实现终端分布匹配。

Method: 将智能体和目标样本分区到空间对应的块中，求解局部传输问题；在质量平衡条件下，限制耦合对全局问题保持可行性并提供Wasserstein成本的上界；通过交替局部分配和控制实现有限时域控制。

Result: 局部分配为有限时域智能体控制生成目标位置，适用于线性和非线性动力学，并建立了逐周期下降保证。

Conclusion: 该框架实现了可扩展的终端分布匹配，同时保持了与Wasserstein目标的严格联系，仿真验证了技术正确性。

Abstract: This paper presents a scalable optimal-transport-based framework for terminal distribution matching in multi-agent systems. While optimal transport provides a natural way to measure distributional mismatch and assign agents to a desired spatial distribution, global discrete transport can become computationally expensive for large-scale systems. We address this bottleneck by partitioning agents and target samples into spatially corresponding blocks and solving smaller local transport problems. Under a mass-balance condition, the resulting restricted coupling remains feasible for the global problem and provides an upper bound on the Wasserstein cost. The local assignments generate target locations for finite-horizon agent control, applicable to both linear and nonlinear dynamics. By alternating local assignment and control, we establish a cycle-to-cycle descent guarantee for the resulting transport surrogate. The proposed framework therefore enables scalable terminal distribution matching while retaining a rigorous connection to the Wasserstein objective. The technical soundness of the proposed results is validated through simulations.

</details>


### [196] [Consensus and Factual Dynamics in Large Populations of Interacting Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39211)
*Emanuele Ricco,Elia Onofri,Vincenzo Sammartino,Roberto Di Pietro*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 研究引入RHEON框架，将LLM代理人群模拟为O(n)自旋系统，探索不同交互拓扑和温度下的共识形成机制，构建了包含470万响应的Eraclitus-4.7M数据集。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 先前研究通常固定单一交互结构，未探讨共识如何依赖于代理之间的交互方式，存在研究空白。

Method: 提出RHEON物理框架，将单一冻结模型的人群模拟为O(n)自旋系统，在从1D环到全耦合平均场的交互几何上演化，以采样温度T为热 disorder源，通过类似Glauber的异步动力学演化。涵盖432种prompt、人群规模、通信拓扑和温度配置。

Result: 代理在最初几次更新扫描中达到最强共识增益；增加每年代理的邻居数量加速收敛；配置是趋向事实正确还是幻觉共识无法仅从初始状态预测；最小化幻觉的温度取决于代理耦合方式，常见的近贪心默认并非最安全；语义一致性与事实收敛正相关，交互增强这种关联，但 unanimous同意仍不足以保证正确性。

Conclusion: LLM代理人群的共识形成受交互拓扑和温度共同影响，近贪心温度不一定是最佳选择， unanimous同意不能作为正确性的可靠指标。

Abstract: Large Language Model (LLM) agents are increasingly deployed as populations of interacting entities, in which consensus --agreement on a shared answer-- emerges as a collective, unengineered behaviour. Prior work on LLM consensus shows that agents can cross-verify their answers and converge towards more factual responses, treating agreement as a proxy for correctness. However, these studies usually fix a single interaction structure, leaving open how consensus depends on how agents interact. We address this gap by introducing RHEON, a physics-inspired framework that recasts a population drawn from a single frozen model as an evolving $O(n)$ spin system on a ladder of interaction geometries of increasing effective dimension --from a 1D ring to a full-coupling mean-field graph-- with the sampling temperature $T$ as the tunable source of thermal disorder, evolved through a Glauber-like asynchronous dynamics. Sweeping RHEON across $432$ configurations of prompt, population size, communication topology, and sampling temperature yields Eraclitus-4.7M, a tagged evolutionary corpus of $4.7$ million responses. We find that agents reach their strongest consensus gain within the first few update sweeps and that increasing the number of neighbours per agent accelerates convergence on average. We further show that whether a configuration settles on factually correct or hallucinated consensus is not predictable from its initial state alone, and that the hallucination-minimising temperature depends on how the agents are coupled, so the common near-greedy default is not automatically the safest. Finally, semantic agreement correlates positively with factual convergence, and interaction strengthens the association, yet never enough for unanimity to certify correctness.

</details>


### [197] [Solving Multi-Agent Sokoban via LaCAM](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39889)
*Keisuke Okumura*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 本文提出了Sokoban-LaCAM，一个利用多智能体路径规划(MAPF)技术解决多智能体推箱问题(Multi-Agent Sokoban)的可扩展规划器，能够在保证完整性和最终最优性的前提下高效处理数十个智能体和箱子的实例。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Sokoban是经典的规划基准问题，但多智能体版本因分支因子爆炸和需处理任务分配与无碰撞路径规划的集成问题而长期发展滞后，缺少实用的多智能体推箱规划器。

Method: 通过借鉴多智能体路径规划(MAPF)领域的最新进展，设计Sokoban-LaCAM规划器，将多智能体推箱问题转化为多智能体路径规划问题进行求解。

Result: Sokoban-LaCAM能够高效解决包含数十个智能体和箱子的多智能体推箱实例，同时保持完整性和最终最优性保证。

Conclusion: MAPF可作为解决更广泛集体自动化问题的强大原语，为仓库物流等实际应用（如自主叉车）提供了有价值的理论基础和技术方案。

Abstract: Sokoban, a puzzle game in which an agent pushes boxes onto unlabelled target locations in a grid world, is a long-standing benchmark planning problem. While it is easy to see the connection to practical applications such as warehouse logistics with autonomous forklifts, its multi-agent counterpart has remained underdeveloped. This is because Multi-Agent Sokoban is substantially more difficult due to factors specific to multi-agent planning, such as the rapidly growing branching factor as the number of agents grows and the need to handle integrated task assignment and collision-free pathfinding. In this paper, we show that a scalable planner for Multi-Agent Sokoban can be designed by leveraging recent advances in multi-agent pathfinding (MAPF). Specifically, our Sokoban-LaCAM efficiently solves instances involving tens of agents and boxes while preserving both completeness and eventual optimality guarantees. This provides evidence that MAPF can serve as a powerful primitive for solving broader collective automation problems.

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [198] [Improving OCR Faithfulness via Gated and Attenuated On-Policy Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38282)
*Baode Wang,Zuming Huang,Kexuan Ren,Jun Huang,Wei Chu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: GAD-RL是一种自适应调节教师监督的视觉-语言模型后训练方法，通过根据学生模型的当前任务性能和局部分布动态调整监督强度，解决固定教师监督随学生提升效果递减的问题，在Qwen3.5-2B上实现59.92%的CHAOS-Bench Micro Recall和91.18的OmniDocBench Overall分数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉-语言模型可能将图像中的异常文本重写为语言上合理的表达，损害OCR转录的忠实度。序列级任务奖励和局部教师指导是互补的，但同一教师的指导效果可能随着学生提升而下降。离线分析表明，固定教师的监督随着学生改进而变得越来越不利。

Method: GAD-RL根据学生当前任务性能和局部分布自适应调节教师监督。冻结的教师以参考转录和学生生成的前缀为条件。对于包含任务奖励≥0.95输出的响应组禁用蒸馏，随组均值奖励增加持续衰减蒸馏强度；按学生對教师Top-1 token的概率加权前向KL，当学生对候选的支持度低时调节局部辅助更新。

Result: 在Qwen3.5-2B上，GAD-RL在CHAOS-Bench上达到59.92%的Micro Recall，比GRPO和GRPO+OPD（固定权重）分别高出8.45和4.43个百分点；在OmniDocBench v1.6上达到91.18的Overall分数。

Conclusion: GAD-RL通过自适应调节教师监督强度，有效解决了视觉-语言模型OCR转录忠实度问题，显著优于现有的固定权重蒸馏方法。

Abstract: Vision-language models may rewrite anomalous text in images into linguistically plausible expressions, compromising OCR transcription faithfulness. Sequence-level task rewards and local teacher guidance are complementary, but guidance from the same teacher may not remain equally effective as the student improves. Offline analysis shows that supervision from a fixed teacher becomes progressively less favorable as the student improves, both across training checkpoints and across response groups with different task rewards. Motivated by this observation, we introduce GAD-RL, which adaptively regulates teacher supervision during joint post-training according to the student's current task performance and local distributions. A frozen teacher conditions on reference transcriptions and student-generated prefixes. GAD-RL disables distillation for response groups containing an output with task reward at least 0.95 and continuously attenuates distillation strength as group-mean reward increases. It also weights forward KL by the student's probability of the teacher's Top-1 token, moderating local auxiliary updates when student support for that candidate is low. On Qwen3.5-2B, GAD-RL achieves 59.92% Micro Recall on CHAOS-Bench, surpassing GRPO and GRPO+OPD (fixed-weight) by 8.45 and 4.43 percentage points, respectively, while achieving an Overall score of 91.18 on OmniDocBench v1.6.

</details>


### [199] [AREX-2: Advancing Self-Improving Agents through Long-Horizon Reflective Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38288)
*Hongjin Qian,Chaofan Li,Kun Luo,Wenqing Wei,Jianlyu Chen,Shuqi Lu,Yuyang Hu,Hongwang Xiao,Hui Wang,Chaozhuo Li,Qiwei Ye,Zhicheng Dou,Defu Lian,Zheng Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: AREX-2是一个基于Qwen3.8-27B的LLM智能体，通过在机器学习和算法编程任务上合成的长程改进轨迹进行训练，实现了自我改进能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 推进LLM智能体的自我改进能力，即在测试时迭代优化解决方案的能力。这一能力依赖于两个互补能力：反思（生成比当前更好的解决方案）和长程执行（多轮保持迭代有效性）。

Method: 假设这两个能力是领域无关的，可以在适合监督的场景中学习。从机器学习和算法编程任务中合成长程改进轨迹（这些领域提供可验证的反馈并奖励持续迭代），在此数据上训练智能体。

Result: 在MLE-bench Lite上达到81.8，Frontier-CS上达70.7；迁移到深度研究任务：BrowseComp 84.0，HLE 52.6，GAIA 92.2，DeepSearchQA 93.8；且随着轮数预算增加持续改进。

Conclusion: 长程反思数据是实现自我改进智能体的有效途径。

Abstract: We present AREX-2, an effort to advance the self-improving capability of LLM agents, which we define as the ability to iteratively refine a solution at test time. This ability rests on two complementary capabilities: reflection, which produces a solution better than the current one, and long-horizon execution, which keeps the iteration effective over many rounds. We hypothesize that both capabilities are domain-agnostic, and can therefore be learned in scenarios that are well suited for supervision. Accordingly, we synthesize long-horizon improvement trajectories from machine learning and algorithmic programming tasks, two domains that offer verifiable feedback and reward sustained iteration. Trained on this data, our agent, built on Qwen3.8-27B, achieves strong results on MLE-bench Lite (81.8) and Frontier-CS (70.7), transfers to deep research with 84.0 on BrowseComp, 52.6 on HLE, 92.2 on GAIA, and 93.8 on DeepSearchQA, and keeps improving as its budget of rounds grows. These results show that long-horizon reflective data is an effective route toward self-improving agents.

</details>


### [200] [MoFlow: Multi-Objective Agentic Workflow Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38294)
*Yining Lu,Aurelie Lozano,Xi Yang,Naoki Abe,Yu Deng,Meng Jiang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出MoFlow，一种基于多目标MDP和凸包蒙特卡洛树搜索的工作流生成方法，能够在单次搜索中覆盖帕累托前沿，从而无需重新训练即可为任意偏好返回最优工作流。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有工作流生成方法通常只优化单一目标或多个目标的加权求和，导致每种训练好的生成器只能适应一种固定偏好。当用户偏好改变时，需要从头重新训练，效率低下。

Method: 将工作流生成建模为多目标马尔可夫决策过程，采用凸包蒙特卡洛树搜索（Convex-Hull MCTS）结合乐观集合值备份策略求解，每个节点存储一组可达的权衡而非单一加权分数，单次搜索即可近似覆盖帕累托前沿。

Result: 在数学、代码和问答三个领域的六个基准数据集上，尽管 baselines 可以为每个测试偏好重新运行（对 MoFlow 不利的设计），MoFlow 仍取得了最高的平均超体积。

Conclusion: MoFlow 能够通过单次搜索覆盖多目标帕累托前沿，实现对任意用户偏好的灵活适配，无需为每个新偏好重新训练模型，为多目标优化工作流生成提供了有效解决方案。

Abstract: We study the generation of agentic workflows that jointly optimize multiple objectives, such as accuracy, cost, latency, robustness, and consistency. Existing methods for workflow generation typically optimize accuracy alone or a weighted sum of objectives, so each trained generator commits to one fixed trade-off and must be retrained from scratch when preferences change. To alleviate this, we propose MoFlow, which generates workflows optimized across varied preferences. Specifically, MoFlow formulates workflow generation as a multi-objective Markov decision process and solves it by leveraging Convex-Hull Monte Carlo Tree Search with optimistic set-valued backups, where every node stores a set of reachable trade-offs rather than one weighted score. A single search thus approximately covers the Pareto front, from which MoFlow can return a workflow for any preference by lookup without retraining. We evaluate MoFlow against six strong baselines on six benchmarks spanning mathematics, code, and question answering. Since the baselines are single-scalar optimizers by design, an apples-to-apples comparison is difficult. We instead adopt an evaluation setup that favors the baselines, in that they are rerun for each testing preference, which MoFlow never sees. Even under this stringent setup, MoFlow achieves the highest average hypervolume.

</details>


### [201] [AI Agents are Vulnerable to Radicalization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38296)
*Ozgur Can Seckin,Shalmoli Ghosh,Alessandro Flammini,Kristina Lerman,Maria Elizabeth Grabe,Filippo Menczer*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Summary generation failed


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Motivation analysis unavailable

Method: Method extraction failed

Result: Result analysis unavailable

Conclusion: Conclusion extraction failed

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) can influence people's beliefs, yet little is known about whether and how they can manipulate each other. To investigate this, we simulate conversations between two agents: a target LLM that role-plays a human persona based on demographic and psychological attributes, and an influencer LLM that aims to make the target's beliefs more extreme. We examine radicalization along two pathways: resonance, where the influencer reinforces a target's pre-existing belief, and persuasion, where the influencer promotes a belief the target initially considers unimportant. Across affective and behavioral metrics, we find that both mechanisms radicalize the target. However, resonance produces consistently stronger effects than persuasion. Different influence tactics, such as using sycophancy and unverified claims, produce different levels of radicalization, but not consistently across metrics. We further show that resonance propagates to related beliefs, suggesting interconnected belief structures within AI agents. These findings indicate that AI agents are susceptible to radicalization, particularly when messages align with their existing beliefs, raising concerns about the vulnerability of personalized AI agents and multi-agent AI ecosystems.

</details>


### [202] [CARAT: Do Materials LLMs Reason or Recite?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38340)
*Jiajun Wu,Jian Yang,Zixiang Ni,Zhenzhu Li,Bin Chong*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: CARAT是一个用于评估材料LLM是否真正从晶体结构推理或只是复制输入中已有答案的基准测试。该研究通过GraphSpace和多种技术手段揭示了LLM在材料科学问答中存在的"作弊"行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准准确率指标无法区分LLM是真正推理晶体结构还是简单复制输入中的答案。研究者希望创建一种方法来严格检验模型的推理能力。

Method: 创建CARAT基准测试，包含8个匹配视图；使用GraphSpace单独命名结构关系；添加匹配微调、答案掩码、证据注入、配对推理和可拒绝回答的规则。

Result: 1. 在最难家族上，基于结构的视图比公式输入高17.3分；2. GraphSpace比普通周期图高19.3分，但其中大部分差距来自基线缺少的内容而非证据呈现方式；3. 一个直接读取列表的快捷规则能回答7个hardened家族中的4个；4. 模型在95.6%的情况下重复关系但不实际使用；5. 监督微调后达99.8%，删除链接后降至23.4%。

Conclusion: 该研究揭示了材料LLM基准测试中存在的重大问题：性能提升主要来自基线方法的不足而非真正的推理能力。研究团队多次重建基准以消除快捷解法，证明模型对链接的依赖和从监督中学习的能力都是可学习的。

Abstract: When a materials LLM answers a question about crystal structure, does it reason from the structure or copy an answer already printed in its input? Accuracy cannot tell: a structural description often prints the very field it is scored against. CARAT holds question and gold answer fixed across eight matched views, names each structural relation separately in GraphSpace, and adds matched fine-tuning, answer masking, evidence injection, paired inference, and a rule that can withhold claims. First, on the benchmark's hardest families the grounded view is worth 17.3 points over formula inputs. Second, we turn that scrutiny on ourselves. GraphSpace beats a plain periodic graph by 19.3 points, but that margin is two effects at once: where the plain rendering carries everything the question needs it is 1.96 points, and where it omits those fields entirely, 46.7 points. The headline mostly measures what the baseline lacked, not how evidence is presented. Third, we attack our own benchmark. A rule that skips the link and reads the list directly answers four of seven hardened families, so we rebuilt it until eleven such shortcuts sat near chance. The frozen model quotes that link yet answers the same when we redirect it, on 95.6% of paired cases: it repeats the relation without using it. After matched supervision it reaches 99.8%, and deleting the link drops it to 23.4%, below the 27.0% the best shortcut reaches: both steps are learnable.

</details>


### [203] [Examining Variation in How Guided AI Tutors Resolve Student Impasses](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38346)
*Bakhtawar Ahtisham,Kirk Vanacore,Alessandra Napoli,Josh Arens,Ksenia Ionova,Clayton Cohn,Shima Salehi,Rene Kizilcec*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本研究分析了AI辅导系统中"帮助困境"问题，通过20,462个学生对话轮次和6,630个僵局轮次，探讨了不同辅导策略对学生学习效果的影响，发现僵局深度增加会降低恢复概率，但直接纠正错误比重复提问更有效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当学生遇到困难时，辅导者面临"帮助困境"：过早帮助会阻碍学生自主探索，过晚帮助则导致学生陷入挫败和困境（wheel spinning）。随着生成式AI辅导工具普及，但缺乏关于僵局持续时AI行为的研究。

Method: 分析1,260个真实辅导会话中的20,462个学生对话轮次，识别6,630个僵局轮次（分为概念错误、表达不确定性、寻求帮助三种类型）。模拟三种辅导条件（baseline、无直接答案、引导式辅导），对150个僵局样本进行提示特异性变化分析，并分析真实交互中的僵局轨迹。

Result: 每增加一个僵局轮次，下一轮恢复的概率降低12.7%（AOR=0.873）。早期辍学学生陷入递归概念激发而未进入执行阶段。提问的益处随僵局持续而衰减（ scripted question × depth AOR=0.78），而纠正学生错误则变得更有益（AOR=1.14）。失败后重复提问恢复率28.1%，而纠正错误恢复率39.8%。

Conclusion: 僵局深度和类型是可观察的轮级对话信号，学习分析系统可利用这些信号实时触发渐进式、状态敏感的辅助策略，优化AI辅导系统的干预时机和方式。

Abstract: When a student is stuck, a tutor faces the assistance dilemma: help given too early can hinder productive struggle, while help withheld too long leaves the student in a frustrating, persistent impasse (i.e., wheel spinning). Generative AI tutors increasingly use guardrails restricting answer-giving, yet little is known about how such tutors behave once an impasse persists. We analyze 20,462 student turns from 1,260 authentic sessions with a guided LLM chemistry tutor, identifying 6,630 impasse turns of three major types: conceptual errors, expressed uncertainty, or help-seeking. We then used these impasses to simulate three tutoring conditions to study variation in AI tutor guidance through impasses: baseline, no-direct-answer, and guided tutor. For a sample of 150 impasses, prompt specificity changed pedagogy: a baseline tutor provided the answer directly in 50.7% of responses, a no-direct-answer tutor asked a follow-up question every time, and the guided tutor responded in a wide variety of ways depending on the context. We then analyzed impasse trajectories in authentic interactions, finding that each additional impasse turn lowered the odds of next-turn recovery by 12.7% (AOR = 0.873, p < .001), and early dropouts were caught in recursive concept elicitation before reaching execution. The benefit of questioning decayed as impasses persisted (scripted question x depth AOR = 0.78; follow-up x depth AOR = 0.83), whereas addressing the student's error grew more beneficial (AOR = 1.14); after a failed scripted question, repeating it was followed by recovery in 28.1% of cases, compared with 39.8% when the tutor addressed the error instead. For learning analytics, these findings identify impasse depth and type as observable, turn-level dialogue signals that analytics can use to trigger graduated, state-sensitive assistance in real time.

</details>


### [204] [Beyond Mode Collapse: Generating Diverse Synthetic Expert Conversations via Generative Flow Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38359)
*Sumit Asthana,Michael Ion,Kevyn Collins Thompson*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出使用生成流网络(GFlowNets)生成多样化高质量合成数据的方法，用于训练后LLM。通过对潜在对话结构进行建模，该方法能够采样专家策略，平衡保真度、模式覆盖和真实性。在辅导和情感支持对话两个领域验证，在三个下游任务上表现优于基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 直接提示LLM或根据终端场景条件生成的数据多样性低，会坍缩到主导模式，无法满足多样化专家策略和决策的需求。

Method: 使用GFlowNets生成潜在对话结构，通过高斯混合密度对关键交互特征（如困惑情节动态、脚手架指令平衡）进行建模，使专家策略采样与其在训练数据中的出现频率成比例。

Result: 在辅导和情感支持对话两个结构不同领域，GFlow合成数据方法在保真度、模式覆盖和真实性方面优于强化学习和端到端LLM基线，且不复制训练数据。在三个下游结果预测任务上，使用GFlow生成对话训练的分类器提供更强的训练信号。

Conclusion: GFlowNets是一种有前景的合成数据生成方法，能够在保持数据质量的同时提高多样性，为下游任务提供更好的训练信号。

Abstract: High quality synthetic data is central to post training LLMs for adaptive AI applications that represent the diverse expert strategies and decisions in conversations. Prompting LLMs directly or conditioning them on end use scenarios yields low diversity data that collapses onto dominant modes. We propose a method to generate diverse high quality synthetic data using Generative Flow Networks (GFlowNets). We show that training GFlowNets to generate latent conversation structure using a Gaussian mixture density over key interaction features (e.g., confusion episode dynamics, scaffolding directive balance) enables sampling expert strategies in proportion to their prevalence in the training data. Across two structurally distinct domains, tutoring and emotional support dialogues, our GFlow based synthetic data generation approach offers a better balance of fidelity, mode coverage and authenticity than reinforcement-learning and end to end LLM baselines, without copying training data. Evaluated on three downstream outcome prediction tasks, classifiers trained on synthetic GFlowNet generated conversations provide a stronger training signal than competitive synthesis baselines.

</details>


### [205] [Can an AI Agent Rediscover a Blaschke-Curve Invariant?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38369)
*Yunus E. Zeytuncu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 研究使用AI代理对四度Blaschke乘积的80个边界配置产生的六对直线进行数值分析，重新发现了一个齐次三次多项式，该多项式能以极高的精度（残差约8.88×10^-17）预测480条直线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索AI辅助数学发现的可能性，在没有告知目标定理的情况下，让AI代理通过数值数据重新发现数学结果。

Method: 固定一个四度Blaschke乘积，AI代理接收80个边界配置产生的六对直线的数值坐标，拟合齐次三次多项式，并使用发现集对角线进行一致性检验。

Result: 拟合的齐次三次多项式能够以RMS残差8.88×10^-17的精度预测480条直线，但单独配置运行报告证据不足，且实验未能确立相比传统多项式拟合的优势。

Conclusion: 该研究提出了一个分离猜想、数值验证和证明的协议框架，但同时指出实验未能证明AI方法优于传统多项式拟合，且存在代理元数据、先验知识和可重复性等方面的明确限制。

Abstract: We study generalized Blaschke curves as a controlled environment for AI-assisted mathematical rediscovery. For one fixed degree-four Blaschke product, an agent receives numerical coordinates of the six pair-lines determined by each of 80 boundary configurations. The target theorem is withheld from the task instructions. The saved research log reports rejected geometric hypotheses and a homogeneous cubic fitted to polygon sides. Its frozen coefficients predict 480 lines from 80 unseen parameter values, with a recorded RMS scale-free residual of $8.88\times10^{{-17}}$. Discovery-set diagonals provide an out-of-fit consistency check, not a fully held-out test. A separate one-configuration run reports insufficient evidence for invariance. A post-review deterministic degree-search baseline also recovers the cubic, so the experiment does not establish an advantage over polynomial fitting. We present this single-instance case study as a protocol for separating conjecture, numerical validation, and proof, with explicit limitations concerning agent metadata, prior knowledge, and reproducibility.

</details>


### [206] [Risk-Aware Adaptive Evaluation: Finding High-Impact Failures Under Limited Budgets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38914)
*Priyanath Maji,Spandan Ghose Chowdhury*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出一种风险感知的上下文汤普森采样策略，用于交互式智能体评估的顺序分配问题，在仅使用6%预算的情况下可发现86%的Impact加权失败案例。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准基准评估采用均匀采样策略，但智能体行为具有随机性，失败案例罕见且重要程度不同。均匀分配导致在不重要场景上浪费评估预算，而关键失败可能被遗漏。

Method: 提出风险感知的上下文汤普森采样策略( Risk-aware Contextual Thompson Sampling)，结合:1)执行前场景上下文向量 2)固定Impact分数 3)评估过程中观察到的失败结果。通过70个τ-bench航空场景和824次试验的离线回放进行测试。

Result: 在最小预算下(仅50次试验，占语料库的6%)：1)恢复86%的oracle Impact加权失败(均匀分配仅25%) 2)发现3.5倍更多的Impact加权失败(215.4 vs 62.2) 3)每美元发现效率提升5倍 4)浪费在从不失败场景的预算从34%降至2.8%。

Conclusion: 风险感知自适应分配在评估预算最稀缺时帮助最大。随着预算增加，优势减小。场景上下文在低预算时帮助最大，而基于后验的探索在中等预算时更有帮助。

Abstract: Evaluating interactive agents is expensive. Agent behavior is stochastic, so reliability must be measured over repeated trials, but failures are rare and differ widely in how much they matter. Standard benchmarks spend this budget uniformly: a read-only lookup is sampled as often as an irreversible payment action. We instead formulate evaluation as a sequential allocation problem. Given a fixed trial budget and a set of scenarios whose failure behavior is unknown, which scenarios should be run, and run again? We propose a risk-aware contextual Thompson Sampling policy that combines a pre-execution scenario context vector and a fixed impact score with the failure outcomes observed during evaluation, and we test it by offline replay over 70 $τ$-bench airline scenarios and 824 recorded trials. Our main result is at the smallest budget: with only 50 trials ($6\%$ of the corpus), the policy recovers $86\%$ of the impact-weighted failures an oracle could find, compared to $25\%$ for uniform allocation. It discovers $3.5\times$ more impact-weighted failures (215.4 vs. 62.2) with the same number of trials, delivers $5\times$ the discovery per dollar, and cuts the budget wasted on scenarios that never fail from $34\%$ to $2.8\%$. The rest of our analysis demonstrates and qualifies this result: a budget sweep shows the advantage shrinks as the budget approaches the corpus size, and paired significance tests show that scenario context helps mainly at small budgets while posterior-based exploration helps at moderate ones. Risk-aware adaptive allocation therefore helps most exactly where evaluation budget is scarcest.

</details>


### [207] [Self-Evolving Harness on Multiple Tasks with the Agent as Its Own Optimizer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38372)
*Qiankai Xu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种自演进harness框架，使用同一冻结模型同时充当solver和proposer，在多个不同领域的基准测试上进行训练和评估。框架将进化过程建模为两阶段深度学习训练（多任务预训练+持续训练），从49行种子harness开始，最终在分布内基准上超越Codex，在分布外基准上与Codex持平。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 1. 现有self-evolving harness方法使用单独的人类设计的harness作为proposer来修改solver的harness；2. 每个基准单独进化harness，缺乏跨领域泛化能力；3. 现实任务来自多个领域，需要harness在多样化任务上具有泛化能力

Method: 1. 同一冻结模型（同一harness版本）同时担任solver和proposer双重角色；2. propoer读取完整运行记录并直接编辑运行它的harness；3. 每轮进化从五个不同领域的基准测试中抽取任务；4. 严格分离训练和测试任务，保留五个分布外基准用于评估；5. 将进化过程建模为两阶段深度学习训练：多任务预训练+持续训练

Result: 第一阶段后：分布内基准平均提升4.48分，分布外基准提升12.64分；在分布内基准上超越Codex，在分布外基准上与Codex持平。第二阶段在Claw-Eval（分布外基准）上继续进化，分数从66.17提升至68.06，超越Codex。进化过程中涌现出多种机制：输出截断、历史压缩、独立审查等。

Conclusion: 1. 同一模型可同时充当solver和proposer实现自演进；2. 多领域训练能产生良好的跨领域泛化能力；3. 两阶段训练范式（预训练+持续训练）有效；4. 从简单49行种子harness可进化出高性能harness；5. 进化过程中自然涌现出有价值的机制

Abstract: A harness is the code around a language-model agent that organizes prompts, calls tools, manages context, and controls execution. As models grow stronger, recent work has begun to let agents improve their own harnesses, a line of work known as self-evolving harnesses. In most existing methods, a separate proposer running on a human-designed harness modifies the solver's harness, and a separate harness is evolved for each benchmark. Real-world tasks come from many domains, so both the evolution and the evaluation of a harness should cover a diverse range of tasks. We propose a framework close to recursive self-improvement: the same frozen model, on the same version of the harness, first solves tasks as the solver and then, as the proposer, reads the complete run records and directly edits the harness that runs it. Each evolution batch draws tasks from five benchmarks in different domains. To measure generalization, training and held-out tasks are strictly separated, and we additionally evaluate on five out-of-distribution benchmarks never used during evolution. We frame the evolution process as deep-learning training with two stages, multi-task pretraining and continual training. Starting from a 49-line seed harness, the harness obtained at the end of the first stage improves the average score by 4.48 points on the in-distribution benchmarks and by 12.64 points on the out-of-distribution benchmarks, surpassing Codex on the former and matching it on the latter. In the second stage, continued evolution on Claw-Eval, one of the out-of-distribution benchmarks, further raises the score on that benchmark from 66.17 to 68.06, exceeding Codex. We also provide an in-depth analysis of the mechanisms that emerged during evolution, including output truncation, history compaction, and independent review.

</details>


### [208] [RefCon: Iterative Refinement and Contrastive Memory Extraction for Context-Evolving Agent](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39143)
*Ubaidillah Ariq Prathama,Bo Liu,Yeo Boon Hong,Yu-Xuan Huang,Yangkai Ding,Tao Yu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出RefCon，通过结合顺序自精炼和并行自对比，在没有金标签的情况下从长时间跨度智能体交互中提取高质量记忆。在AppWorld、BFCL-V3等多个数据集上取得显著提升，相对基线提升达21.6%（ACE）和16.6%（ReMe），多样性变体DivCon在ReasoningBank上提升35.5%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长时间跨度智能体交互产生有用但有噪声的经验数据，而重新训练模型吸收这些数据的成本很高。上下文演化的智能体需要能够在测试时利用更多计算资源提升性能的记忆提取方法，且不依赖金标签。

Method: 提出RefCon方法，结合顺序自精炼（sequential self-refinement）与并行自对比（parallel self-contrast），在无金标签条件下提取更高质量的记忆。

Result: 在AppWorld和BFCL-V3上评估，RefCon在多种上下文演化智能体框架中带来一致且显著的提升：ACE上相对提升21.6%，ReMe上相对提升16.6%。多样性聚焦变体DivCon在ReasoningBank上取得35.5%的提升。在软件工程任务中甚至超越金标签基线。

Conclusion: RefCon无需金标签即可持续超越基线方法，并能跨模型规模泛化。分析表明RefCon保持良好的效率-准确性权衡，且随着更多轨迹使用持续改进，而单纯多样性扩展较早饱和。

Abstract: Long-horizon agent interactions generate useful but noisy experience, and retraining models to absorb it is expensive. Context-evolving agents therefore need memory extraction methods that improve with more test-time compute without relying on gold labels. We propose RefCon, which combines sequential self-refinement with parallel self-contrast to extract higher-quality memories without gold labels. Evaluated on AppWorld and BFCL-V3 across multiple context-evolving agent frameworks, RefCon delivers strong and consistent gains, including relative improvements of 21.6% on ACE and 16.6% on ReMe over no-scaling baselines, while a diversity-focused variant (DivCon) achieves a 35.5% gain on ReasoningBank. RefCon consistently outperforms existing baselines without ground-truth labels, and generalizes across model scales and to software engineering tasks, where it surpasses even ground-truth baselines. We further analyze the accuracy-token trade-off and scaling behavior, showing RefCon maintains favorable efficiency and continues to improve as more trajectories are used, unlike diversity-only scaling which saturates earlier.

</details>


### [209] [Aligned Data Can Induce Misalignment via Context Confusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38379)
*Yavuz Bakman,Duygu Nur Yaldiz,Baris Askin,Swastik Roy,Morteza Ziyadi,Salman Avestimehr,Sai Praneeth Karimireddy*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出并定义了"上下文混淆"(context confusion)现象：当大型语言模型在特定领域进行对齐训练后，模型可能在其他相关但上下文不同的领域表现出未对齐行为。例如，在研究数据保存场景中推荐保存数据是对齐的，但在移动应用隐私场景中推荐保存用户敏感数据则可能是不恰当的。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前的对齐训练方法假设对齐是上下文无关的，但实际上对齐是上下文相关的。论文旨在揭示对齐训练可能在不同上下文中引发误对齐的问题。

Method: 论文在三个领域（性别平等、隐私、物理安全）中进行实验验证上下文混淆现象；对比通用对齐数据和针对性对齐数据的效果；使用上下文学习示例进行推理；分析模型表征变化以解释机制。

Result: 上下文混淆确实存在于三个领域中；这是一种"狭窄误对齐"（narrow misalignment），而非"新兴误对齐"（emergent misalignment）；通用对齐数据无法有效减少该现象，但针对性对齐数据或上下文学习示例可显著减少；机制上，不同领域的查询在微调过程中经历了相似的表征迁移，导致行为特征跨上下文激活。

Conclusion: 仅通过检查训练数据难以预测模型训练后的对齐状态，这凸显了全面进行训练后对齐评估的重要性。研究建议在部署前需要对模型进行全面的上下文相关对齐评估。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are frequently updated for various use cases, where filtering out misaligned training samples is a common practice for preventing post-update misalignment. However, alignment is inherently context-dependent: a recommendation that is aligned in one context may be inappropriate in another. For example, in response to the question "What should a researcher do with the research data?", recommending that the researcher preserve the data for reproducibility is aligned. In contrast, recommending data saving in response to "What should a mobile-app developer do with users' sensitive data?" may be inappropriate from a privacy perspective. Starting from this observation, we identify a post-training phenomenon where aligned training induces misaligned behavior in other contexts. We call this phenomenon **context confusion**. We demonstrate context confusion across three domains: (1) Gender Equality, (2) Privacy, and (3) Physical Safety. We further show that context confusion causes narrow misalignment, in contrast to emergent misalignment, and is not effectively reduced by injecting general alignment data, but can be substantially reduced by including targeted alignment data for the misaligned domain or providing in-context learning examples during inference. Lastly, we provide a mechanistic explanation of *context confusion*. We observe that queries from different domains can undergo similar representational shifts during the fine-tuning. Consequently, a query from a different domain may activate the same behavioral feature learned during fine-tuning, which causes the behavior to transfer to a context where it is misaligned. Based on our findings, we argue that it is difficult to predict the alignment state of a model after training by inspecting the training data alone, which highlights the importance of comprehensive post-training alignment evaluations.

</details>


### [210] [OverForge: Reasoning Through Strategies and Tactics Helps Cooperative Lifelong Adaptation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39727)
*Oana Madalina Fron,Ojas Shirekar,Chirag Raman*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: OverForge是一种无需训练的分层架构，将协作语言模型智能体的战略推理（角色和分工）与战术推理（行动执行）分离，通过元认知前额叶皮层模块耦合两层并形成策略-行动分支、想象后果并自信决策。在OvercookedV2基准测试中，OverForge完成7份汤vs基线3份，保留约定角色并适应新伙伴。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 协作语言模型智能体需要在长时域中协调，适应变化的环境和不熟悉的伙伴，但现有智能体将观察映射到行动时未将持久协调策略与战术执行分离。

Method: 提出OverForge，一种无需训练的分层架构，分离战略推理（角色和分工）与战术推理（通过私人、伙伴条件的世界模型执行行动）。元认知前额叶皮层模块通过形成策略-行动分支、用前向模型想象后果并在自信时执行来耦合两层。
