Result: 当前最先进的VLMs配合强大的智能体框架在解决科学领域关键问题方面仍面临挑战；跨多语言、推理能力、上下文长度等因素的基准测试结果分析得出若干重要结论和方向。

Conclusion: 提供了一个连接专家定义科学目标与可验证软件结果的集成框架，支持对科学工作流中智能体能力和智能体框架设计的系统评估，为未来发展指明了重要方向。

Abstract: Scientific software presents a demanding test for computer-using agents based on visual language models (VLMs): completing a research workflow requires interpreting specialized interfaces, manipulating scientific objects, and producing verifiable results. We thus introduce OSWorld-Science, a benchmark and evaluation environment that combines scientifically meaningful tasks, artifact-based evaluation, and an efficient agent harness for studying computer use in the scientific domain. The benchmark contains 12 VLMs and 146 high-quality tasks across several scientific domains and software configurations, covering workflows such as molecular drawing and retrosynthesis, pathology image analysis, statistical computing, and physical simulation. Tasks are developed through expert proposals and iterative human--AI co-design, with selection guided by scientific value and difficulty. Task-specific execution-based evaluators inspect application states and generated artifacts, including molecular structures, segmentation masks, plots, and numerical results, and award partial credit for incomplete outcomes. Our special harness integrates model adapters, interaction-loop control, and trajectory logging to support comparisons of models and interaction strategies. Our results show that current state-of-the-art VLMs with a strong harness still face challenges in addressing key questions in the scientific domains. We also analyze the benchmarking results across multi-linguistics, reasoning efforts, context length and other factors and derive several important conclusions and directions to assist future development. Overall, we provide an integrated framework connecting expert-defined scientific goals to verifiable software outcomes, enabling systematic evaluation of both agent capabilities and harness design in scientific workflows.

</details>


### [317] [ConflictGuide: AutoResearch Improves When Competing Behaviors Are Made Visible](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39933)
*Binqian Xu,Qiran Zou,Xiangbo Shu,Dianbo Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出ConflictGuide系统，解决LLM-based AutoResearch系统中竞争行为权衡问题。传统标量反馈仅关注任务性能，忽略编辑如何影响多个竞争行为。ConflictGuide通过文献支持的分类法和模型特定证据识别竞争行为，并设计探针进行测量。采用两阶段演化：阶段I使用任务反馈探索，阶段II使用探针反馈引导向冲突缓解方向发展。在五个不同模型家族中，ConflictGuide相比纯标量反馈的AutoResearch，最多可减少28%的任务错误和14%的冲突相关错误。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器学习模型设计中，不同属性之间经常存在冲突：提升某一行为可能损害另一行为。当前基于LLM的AutoResearch系统主要依赖标量任务性能反馈，忽略了这种权衡问题。标量反馈虽支持早期广泛探索，但无法揭示编辑如何影响竞争行为，导致系统难以在多行为冲突时取得进展。

Method: 提出ConflictGuide系统及其可重用的ConflictGuide-Skill组件。该方法结合文献支持的分类法和模型特定证据，识别竞争行为并指定探针，供代码代理实现为度量指标。演化过程分两阶段：阶段I使用任务反馈进行探索；阶段II使用探针反馈引导提案朝向冲突缓解方向，仅当探针显示足够缓解时才保留边际增益编辑。

Result: 在五个不同模型家族中进行匹配预算实验，结果显示：引入竞争行为反馈后，随着任务收益递减，提高两种行为的提案比例增加，超越纯标量反馈的瓶颈。ConflictGuide相比纯标量反馈的AutoResearch，最多可减少28%的任务错误和14%的冲突相关错误，且收益可扩展到其他代码代理。

Conclusion: 竞争行为反馈在任务收益递减时引入，可增加同时改善两种行为的提案比例，并维持超越纯标量反馈的进展。ConflictGuide通过识别竞争行为并设计探针测量，为代码代理提供可操作的反馈，有效解决多行为权衡问题，具有良好的可扩展性和通用性。

Abstract: When designing machine learning models, desirable properties are often in tension: improving one behavior can impair another, so task progress can depend on alleviating the conflict. LLM-based AutoResearch systems, which iteratively edit model code and retain edits based on scalar task-performance feedback, have largely ignored this trade-off. We find that scalar feedback supports broad exploration early in search, but it does not reveal how edits affect competing behaviors. In matched-budget experiments, introducing competing-behavior feedback as task gains diminish increases the share of proposals that improve both behaviors and sustains progress beyond scalar-only plateaus. Obtaining this feedback for a given model requires identifying its competing behaviors and designing probes to measure them. To make competing-behavior feedback actionable, we introduce ConflictGuide. Its reusable ConflictGuide-Skill combines a literature-grounded taxonomy with model-specific evidence to identify competing behaviors and specify probes for a code agent to implement as metrics. Evolution proceeds in two stages: Stage I explores with task feedback; Stage II uses probe feedback to steer proposals toward conflict alleviation and retains marginal-gain edits only when probes indicate sufficient alleviation. Across five diverse model families, ConflictGuide reduces task and conflict-related errors by up to 28% and 14%, respectively, relative to scalar-only AutoResearch, with gains extending to other code agents.

</details>


### [318] [Coverage Before Control: Route-Instruction Grounding and Steering for Controllable Retrosynthesis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39955)
*Xuemin Chen,Xiaozhuang Song,Xinjian Zhao,Yaoyao Xu,Tianshu Yu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出RIGS框架，用于指令条件下的逆合成预测，解决化学家在多个前体集合中选择时的偏好问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有单步逆合成模型仅评估恢复记录反应的能力，但实际中化学家可能需要在多个前体集合中选择以保留特定结构片段。现有方法无法同时满足相关替代方案的覆盖率和偏好控制。

Method: 提出两阶段框架RIGS：阶段A训练语言投影器，学习指令偏好的替代方案；阶段B使用投影器通过轻量级残差适配器引导冻结的生成模型。构建嵌套的一对多训练支持，将每个产品与不断增加的候选前体集合配对。

Result: 更广泛的支持帮助模型生成更多样化的替代方案；RIGS能够学习根据指令引导生成；覆盖率和控制之间的关系在不同模型规模上一致但呈非单调变化。

Conclusion: RIGS框架成功实现了指令条件下的逆合成预测，平衡了替代方案的覆盖率和用户偏好控制，为化学逆合成提供了更灵活的预测工具。

Abstract: Single-step retrosynthesis models are commonly evaluated by their ability to recover recorded reactions. In practice, chemists may need to choose among several precursor sets for the same product, for example to preserve a particular motif. Recovering a recorded answer alone does not establish this ability to follow a preference. Satisfying such requests requires both coverage of relevant alternatives and control over which alternatives are favored. We introduce Route-Instruction Grounding and Steering (RIGS), a two-stage framework for instruction-conditioned retrosynthesis. Stage A trains a language projector, teaching it which alternatives an instruction favors or discourages. Stage B uses the projector learned in Stage A to steer a frozen generative model through lightweight residual adapters. We construct nested one-to-many training supports by pairing each product with increasing numbers of candidate precursor sets. Extensive experiments demonstrate that broader support helps the model generate a wider range of alternatives, and RIGS can learn to guide generation according to instructions. The relationship between coverage and control is consistent across model scales but non-monotone.

</details>


### [319] [Better Deck or Different Judge? Evaluating Agentic Harness Gains in Corporate and Investment Banking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39958)
*Ludovic Gibert,Matis Despujols,Andre-Louis Rochet*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文回顾性研究了一个结合27B语言模型、金融计算、叙事模板和验证检查的智能体框架在企业及投资银行演示文稿生成中的应用。该系统相比直接使用短提示的模型在五个评判者的评分中高出20.4-33.6分（满分95分），但重复评分会导致分数漂移，使小型改进难以与评判者变异性区分开来。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 企业及投资银行团队需要制作演示文稿来支持信贷决策和客户交易建议，这一过程需要协调财务数据、追踪来源并将分析转化为建议。研究旨在开发一个自动化系统来提高这一工作的效率和质量。

Method: 研究采用回顾性方法，开发了一个结合27B语言模型、金融计算、叙事模板和验证检查的智能体框架（agentic harness）。使用LLM评判器来指导工程变更并评估生成的演示文稿。在移除模板标记的共享会话纯文本评分中，由五位评判者对系统输出进行评估。

Result: 五位评判者给完整系统的评分比同一模型直接使用短提示生成的结果高出20.4至33.6分（满分95分）。每位评判者在全部17个开发交付物上都给系统打了更高分。与使用短提示直接生成Opus相比，分数差距从-4.7到+0.8分不等。评判者在开发轮次的大致进展上达成一致，但对最终演示文稿排名的共识低于对综合分数的共识。重复评分也会对未更改的演示文稿产生分数漂移。

Conclusion: LLM评判器可有效指导系统开发，但存在局限性：评判者一致性在最终排名上较低，重复评分会导致分数变化，使得小型改进难以与评判者变异性区分开来。提出了一个重要问题：更高的分数是否真正反映更好的文档，还是评分标准本身发生了变化。

Abstract: Corporate and investment banking teams use presentations to support credit decisions and advise clients on financing and transactions. Producing these decks requires reconciling financial data, tracing sources and turning analysis into a recommendation. We retrospectively study the development of an agentic harness combining a 27B language model, financial calculations, narrative templates and validation checks. LLM judges guide engineering changes and assess the resulting decks, raising the question of whether higher scores reflect better documents or changes in grading. In shared-session text-only grading with template markers removed, five judges score the complete system 20.4 to 33.6 points out of 95 above the same model generating directly from a short prompt. Every judge scores the system higher on all seventeen development deliverables. Margins against direct Opus generation from a short prompt range from -4.7 to +0.8 points. Judges agree on broad progress across development rounds but agree less on final-deck rankings than on pooled scores. Repeated grading also shifts scores on unchanged decks, making small improvements difficult to distinguish from judge variability.

</details>


### [320] [What Can Component-Replacement Evidence Establish? A Critical Scoping Review of Local Decisions in LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39989)
*Shuyang Zhang,Jianshuo Chang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 这是一篇关于语言模型代理中组件替换的关键范围综述，研究了348项研究并检查了90条比较记录，旨在区分组件替换的任务级收益与本地决策质量的贡献。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语言模型代理中的组件替换会改变执行轨迹，可能影响后续观察、资源使用和恢复机会。需要不同证据来评估其任务级收益和本地决策质量的贡献，但现有研究缺乏对这两者的清晰区分。

Method: 关键范围综述方法，绘制348项研究图谱，检查90条比较记录（88条来自40项纳入研究，2条来自补充研究），并通过8个有目的选取的案例围绕替换决策、执行条件、测量可比性、控制和剩余解释进行结构化综合。

Result: 222项研究报告了本地决策指标，其中142项同时报告了测量的任务终点，49项报告了代理。Outcome Monitors报告了包级完成增益，但其归因于检测器质量的能力仍然有限；First-chunk selection报告了针对离线代理端点的局部改进；Evidence-Carrying Termination报告了更少的过早无支持终止和非劣效完成，但未确立完成优越性。跨案例分析确定了三种涉及恢复和破坏、干预时机及下游使用的候选机制。

Conclusion: 综述区分了组件替换的任务级收益与本地决策质量的贡献，并推导出了八项具体报告项目。在线执行或本地和任务指标的同时增益都不能单独确立更好的本地决策解释了任务级收益。归因和部署取决于比较控制、标签定义和控制器可用的信息。

Abstract: Background. A component replacement in a language-model agent changes an execution trajectory, potentially altering later observations, resource use, and recovery opportunities. Different evidence is needed to assess its task-level benefit and the contribution of local decision quality. Methods. This critical scoping review maps 348 studies and examines 90 comparison records: 88 from 40 included studies and two from supplementary studies. Eight purposively selected cases structure the synthesis around the replaced decision, executed conditions, measurement comparability, controls, and remaining explanations. Results. Of 222 studies reporting local decision metrics, 142 also report measured task endpoints and 49 report proxies. These counts identify studies that report both types of measurement, without establishing that the measurements come from matched comparisons. Outcome Monitors reports a package-level completion gain whose attribution to detector quality remains limited; First-chunk selection reports a local improvement assessed against an offline proxy endpoint; Evidence-Carrying Termination reports fewer premature unsupported terminations and completion non-inferiority, without establishing completion superiority. Cross-case analysis identifies three candidate mechanisms involving recovery and disruption, intervention timing, and downstream use. Attribution and deployment depend on the comparison controls, label definitions, and information available to the controller. Conclusions. The review distinguishes the task-level benefit of a component replacement from the contribution of local decision quality and derives eight claim-specific reporting items. Neither online execution nor simultaneous gains in local and task metrics alone establish that better local decisions explain the task-level gain.

</details>


### [321] [Who Verifies the Graph? Misspecification Attacks on Causal Action Verification for Language Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.40027)
*Fabio Rovai*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究因果行动验证器(CIVeX)的安全性，通过对抗性测试发现其证书机制存在漏洞，并提出审计方法来检测错误行动，但错误不作为仍需额外成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: CIVeX声称在混淆工具使用基准上零错误执行,但作者试图通过仅破坏承诺图来对其进行红队测试,验证其安全性主张。

Method: 通过两种攻击方式测试CIVeX:(1)省略单条双向边;(2)反转箭头使中介变量被误标为混淆变量。使用有界随机样本的验证步骤检测攻击,并审计拒绝执行的操作。

Result: 省略一条双向边使错误执行从0%升至15.3%,91%为有害执行,效用从+2.27降至+0.35。反转箭头后48.9%错误执行且无正确执行。验证步骤检测到两种攻击,但97.1%的有益操作仍被拒绝。恢复安全需127次实验,恢复价值需额外614次。

Conclusion: 仅检查执行的审计可防止错误行动,但错误不作为需单独付费;证书机制虽内部有效但无法保证图规范正确性,审计成本与拒绝数量而非执行数量成正比。

Abstract: Causal action verifiers gate an agent's state-changing tool calls by checking whether each proposed intervention is identifiable against a committed action-state graph, and they issue a certificate that carries the identification argument and a one-sided lower confidence bound. One such verifier, CIVeX, reports zero false executions on a confounded tool-use benchmark. We red-team it by corrupting only the committed graph. Omitting a single bidirected edge takes it from zero false executions to 15.3% at the benchmark's published confounding strength, with 91% of its executions harmful and utility falling from +2.27 to +0.35. Reversing one arrowhead, so that a mediator is committed as a confounder, gives 48.9% false executions and no correct ones. Every one of these actions carries an internally valid certificate. An attestation step that tests each observationally certified execution against a bounded randomised sample detected both attacks, with 2 false alarms in 555 executions on a truthful graph; refusing what fails the test, or cannot be tested, gave zero false executions in every setting we measured. It does not restore beneficial execution: at the published strength 97.1% of beneficial actions are still never executed, because the same misspecification rejects them before attestation runs. Those rejections carry certificates too, and auditing them works, but its cost scales with the number of rejections rather than the number of executions. Recovering safety costs 127 experiments per 1,050 actions; recovering the lost value costs 614 more, at which point the audited verifier makes the honest graph's decisions on every instance and spends exactly its experiment budget. An audit that inspects only executions protects against wrongful action. Wrongful inaction has to be paid for separately.

</details>


### [322] [PTNO: Training Neural Operators with Noisy Monte Carlo Estimates for Particle Transport Problems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.40090)
*Yubo Cao,Xi Deng,Mengqi Xia,Vignesh Gopakumar,Ander Gray,Anima Anandkumar*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: PTNO是一种神经算子，能够直接从噪声、低成本的蒙特卡洛标签学习粒子传输代理模型，无需昂贵的收敛解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 粒子传输的高保真蒙特卡洛模拟成本过高，现有学习式代理模型需要基于昂贵的收敛解训练，提出从噪声MC标签直接学习的低成本方案。

Method: 1) 通过大量噪声场景训练分散MC成本实现泛化；2) 使用物理空间标签+softplus层保证正值；3) 采用逐点相对L2损失(PRelL2)处理高动态范围

Result: 中子输运任务：比收敛MC快10^4-10^5倍，便宜10^3-10^5倍；辐射传输任务：MC成本为PTNO的0.8-11倍

Conclusion: PTNO成功实现了从噪声MC标签学习高质量粒子传输代理模型，在保持精度的同时大幅降低计算成本，为HDR场景的神经网络训练提供了有效方法。

Abstract: Particle transport under multiple scattering is central to radiative transfer and plasma physics, yet high-fidelity Monte Carlo (MC) simulations must trace prohibitively many particles. Learning-based surrogates can amortize this cost, but typically train on expensive, well-converged MC solutions. We propose the Particle Transport Neural Operator (PTNO), a neural operator that learns particle transport surrogates directly from noisy, low-cost MC labels. Such labels pose two challenges: (1) high variance, which destabilizes standard supervised learning, and (2) a high dynamic range (HDR) spanning many orders of magnitude. For the first, we learn the solution operator from noisy labels of many configurations, amortizing MC cost and generalizing to unseen configurations. Because MC labels are unbiased, we show that the squared loss on them shares its minimizer with the loss on converged solutions, and our budget-allocation study over training scenes $M$, MC samples per render $N$, and independent renders per scene $K$ shows that many noisy scenes beat fewer converged ones. For the second, a nonlinear transform such as the logarithm biases noisy supervision. Instead, PTNO keeps labels in physical space and enforces positivity with a softplus output layer that represents small values effectively. We further train with a pointwise relative $L_2$ loss (PRelL2), the stop-gradient relative loss of HDR denoising and neural rendering, which normalizes each residual by the stop-gradient prediction instead of the noisy label. We demonstrate PTNO on neutron transport in fusion reactors and radiative transfer in participating media. On the two neutronics tasks, PTNO is $10^4$-$10^5\times$ faster than converged MC on the same CPU and $10^3$-$10^5\times$ cheaper than MC at matched accuracy; on the two radiative-transfer tasks, MC at matched accuracy costs $0.8$-$11\times$ as much as PTNO.

</details>


### [323] [Agent Error Dataset: Scaling 50,000 Error--Diagnosis Pairs for Failure Analysis and Error-Aware Post-Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.40111)
*Kunlun Zhu,Xuyan Ye,Yibo Li,Cheng Qian,Beibin Li,Heng Ji*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出了Agent错误数据集(AED)，包含50,228个错误诊断对，来自9,961个源任务、33个环境、19个测试框架家族和23个策略模型。提出AET管道将自然失败转化为诊断和纠正建议，在3,062个匹配重放对中，首轮建议将验证器通过率从18.4%提升至51.1%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前LLM agent部署失败时仅有最终奖励信号，缺乏诊断信息。论文认为失败过程本身包含丰富的学习信号，可用于识别需要修订的决策并测试具体替代方案，从而实现经验复用。

Method: 构建五阶段AET管道：(1)收集自然失败 (2)生成诊断和纠正建议 (3)对照记录证据验证 (4)支持重放比较 (5)分别构建诊断和actor恢复的训练视图。保留源追踪和执行元数据支持跨设置失败分析。

Result: 在3,062个匹配重放对中，首轮纠正建议将验证器通过率从18.4%提升至51.1%，提升32.7个百分点。Qwen3-8B全诊断微调使与内部教师标签的精确步骤一致率从47.2%提升至63.6%。Actor训练中，action-only repair训练比success-only训练在WebShop-lite上高6.67个百分点。

Conclusion: 错误诊断和纠正建议可有效用于训练：保留执行元数据的错误数据集支持诊断和actor恢复训练，能显著提升LLM agent在文本agent系统中的性能。

Abstract: An unsuccessful LLM agent rollout contains more information than its final reward: the observations available to the agent, the actions it chose, and the environment's responses. Reusing this experience for learning requires identifying a decision to revise and testing a concrete alternative. We introduce the Agent Error Dataset (AED), comprising 50,228 error-diagnosis pairs from 9,961 source tasks across 33 environments, 19 harness families, and 23 policy models in text-based agent systems. We retain source traces and execution metadata to support cross-setting failure analysis and re-diagnosis without repeating the original rollout. Our five-stage Agentic Error-to-Training (AET) pipeline collects natural failures, generates diagnoses and proposed corrections, and checks them against recorded evidence. Where replay is supported, we compare corrections with original-action retries from the same checkpoint under matched execution settings. We then construct separate training views for diagnosis and actor recovery. Across 3,062 matched replay pairs, first-proposal corrections raise verifier pass rates from 18.4% to 51.1%, a gain of 32.7 percentage points. Using a separately frozen diagnosis release, full-diagnosis fine-tuning on 1,656 source tasks raises Qwen3-8B's exact-step agreement with internal teacher labels from 47.2% to 63.6%, averaged over three seeds on a 943-case holdout. The strongest prompted reference in this comparison scores 54.7%, and mean agreement improves at each of four increasing training-set sizes. In a single-seed comparison of actor-training recipes, action-only repair training scores 6.67 percentage points higher on WebShop-lite than success-only training.

</details>


### [324] [Unlearnable, or Unmeasured? On the Reliability of Difficulty Labels in RLVR](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.40115)
*Chandak Chakma,Syed Nazmus Sakib,Nafiul Haque,Shifat E. Arman*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文重新审视了强化学习可验证奖励（RLVR）中困难提示"不可学习"的现象，发现这些提示实际上可以学习，只是学习速度约为可学习提示的三分之一，且用于定义困难提示的标签比预期更不可复现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 近期研究表明部分困难提示即使偶尔产生正确解决方案也难以学习，作者想验证这一"不可学习"现象是否真实存在，并探究其原因。

Method: 1. 重新评估困难提示的学习情况；2. 分析困难标签在不同种子和采样次数下的可复现性；3. 开发采样框架量化这种不稳定性；4. 重新分析梯度相似性证据，将困难提示因正确 rollout 较少导致的梯度估计差异纳入考虑。

Result: 困难提示确实能以约三分之一的可学习速率改进；困难标签的可复现性远低于预期，不同种子组合会改变所选提示而非仅减少测量噪声；匹配样本数量后梯度差异减弱但未完全消失。

Conclusion: 慢学习现象在重新分析后仍然成立，但定义该现象的提示和解释它的证据都需要更仔细的测量。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards (RLVR) has become an important approach for improving reasoning during post-training. Recent work suggests that some difficult prompts remain resistant to learning even when they occasionally produce correct solutions. We revisit this unlearnability phenomenon and find that the affected prompts do improve, at roughly one third of the learnable rate, while the difficulty-defined set used to study them is much less reproducible than expected. These difficulty labels are estimated from a limited number of sampled responses. Combining them across seeds can further change which prompts are selected instead of simply reducing measurement noise. We develop a sampling-based framework for quantifying this instability and determining how much evaluation is required for difficulty assignments to reproduce reliably. We also revisit the gradient-similarity evidence proposed to explain unlearnability and show that part of the observed separation arises because difficult prompts provide fewer correct rollouts from which their gradients can be estimated. Matching this sample count weakens the gradient difference but does not remove it. Overall, the slow-learning phenomenon survives our reanalysis, while both the prompts used to define it and the evidence used to explain it require more careful measurement.

</details>


### [325] [Learning from Research: Toward Lifelong Agent Harness Evolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.40169)
*Jingbo Yang,Kwei-Herng Lai,Xiaowen Wang,Yaar Harari,Evgeniy Gabrilovich,Shiyu Chang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: ScholarEvolve是一个通过自动整合最新研究文献来指导语言智能体 harness 演化的框架，突破传统方法仅依赖执行反馈的局限性，实现智能体的主动 lifelong evolution。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前语言智能体需要解决越来越复杂的任务，但现有方法依赖元编码智能体基于执行反馈修改harness，这种方式受限于智能体已有知识和被动观察失败，导致探索受限且具有反应性。作者希望通过借鉴人类专家从研究文献中学习新方案的方式来解决这个问题。

Method: ScholarEvolve将harness演化方向组织为功能模块，使用主题建模识别每个模块的不同改进策略，实现这些策略并评估其组合以提升任务性能。该框架设计可随时间纳入新出版物，使研究进展驱动主动的终身演化。

Result: 在AppWorld和Tau2-Bench上展示改进。ScholarEvolve将Qwen3.5-27B在AppWorld Challenge上的任务目标完成率从49.6%提升至63.6%，将GPT-5.4-mini在Tau2-Bench Telecom上的pass@1从72.7%提升至81.9%。

Conclusion: ScholarEvolve成功证明了利用最新研究文献指导智能体harness演化的有效性，为语言智能体的持续改进提供了一种新的主动式而非被动反应式的方法，具有重要的应用价值。

Abstract: Language agents are expected to solve increasingly complex tasks, creating a growing need for continual improvement. One promising approach is to evolve the agent harness, the software that governs tool use, memory management, and task execution, while keeping the underlying language model fixed. Recent methods automate this process by using a meta coding agent to modify the harness based on execution feedback. However, relying on that agent's existing knowledge and observed failures can restrict exploration and make adaptation reactive. Inspired by how human experts learn from the research literature for new solutions, we introduce ScholarEvolve, a framework that automatically draws on state-of-the-art research to guide harness evolution. ScholarEvolve organizes the harness evolution directions into functional modules and uses topic modeling to identify distinct improvement strategies for each module. It implements these strategies and evaluates their combinations to improve task performance. Moreover, the framework is designed to incorporate new publications over time, allowing research advances to drive proactive lifelong evolution. Experiments demonstrate improvements on AppWorld and Tau2-Bench. ScholarEvolve raises Qwen3.5-27B task goal completion from 49.6% to 63.6% on AppWorld Challenge, and raises GPT-5.4-mini pass@1 from 72.7% to 81.9% on Tau2-Bench Telecom.

</details>


### [326] [PivotOPD: Learning to Recover from Pivotal Mistakes in Multi-Turn Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.40285)
*Yinghui He,Yapei Chang,Khushi Bhardwaj,Daniele Molinari,Tugrul Konuk,Jan Kautz,Ali Hatamizadeh*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: PivotOPD是一种新型的策略蒸馏框架，用于训练语言代理，通过识别多轮交互中的"关键错误"并同时训练学生预防和恢复这些错误。在ALFWorld、WebShop和Search-based QA上相对于13个基线取得了最佳平均性能，并在SWE-Bench Verified上展现了跨模型家族的迁移能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有策略蒸馏方法在多轮交互中存在错误累积问题：早期的一个错误会改变后续状态，导致后续动作也失败。关键发现是大多数失败 rollout 包含一个关键错误（pivotal mistake），且通常发生在早期，但这些错误往往是可以恢复的，只需要指导几轮就能恢复任务成功。

Method: 1) 识别关键错误：在每个 rollout 中检测使代理远离任务完成的动作；2) 预防性蒸馏（Preventive Distillation）：使用逆向KL散度，让学生远离关键错误；3) 恢复性蒸馏（Recovery Distillation）：使用正向KL散度，迁移学生很少采样的恢复行为；4) 联合训练：同时进行预防和恢复训练

Result: 1) 在ALFWorld、WebShop、Search-based QA上，Qwen3-1.7B和Qwen3-8B学生均取得最强平均性能；2) 在ALFWorld上比最强基线提升+5.5%（1.7B学生）；3) 跨模型家族迁移：在SWE-Bench Verified上，Nemotron-3.5学生的解决率提升+3.2%

Conclusion: PivotOPD通过同时解决关键错误的预防和恢复问题，显著提升了语言代理在多轮交互任务中的表现，且具有跨模型家族的泛化能力。

Abstract: On-policy distillation (OPD) is a promising approach for training language agents, providing dense teacher supervision on student-generated trajectories. However, in multi-turn interaction, an incorrect action changes the states the student encounters later, so errors compound across turns. In preliminary experiments across three Qwen3 models (8B to 235B), we find that more than half of the failed rollouts contain a pivotal mistake, an action that moves the agent farther from completing the task, and this mistake typically occurs early. These pivotal mistakes often remain recoverable: guiding the model for only a few turns after the pivotal turn can restore task success. We therefore propose PivotOPD, an on-policy distillation framework that jointly trains the student to prevent pivotal mistakes and to recover from the states they create. At each pivotal mistake, a teacher model provides a gold action and then names a recovery action at each of the next few turns. Preventive distillation uses the gold action with reverse KL to steer the student away from the pivotal mistake, while recovery distillation uses the recovery actions with forward KL to transfer recovery behaviors that the student rarely samples. Against 13 baselines on ALFWorld, WebShop, and Search-based QA, PivotOPD achieves the strongest average performance for both Qwen3-1.7B and Qwen3-8B students, improving over the strongest baseline on ALFWorld by +5.5% with the 1.7B student. The gains also transfer to another model family on the software engineering domain, where PivotOPD raises the resolve rate of a Nemotron-3.5 student on SWE-Bench Verified by +3.2%. Project page: https://research.nvidia.com/labs/lpr/pivotopd/

</details>


### [327] [How Much of a Harness Does a Strong Agent Need for Autonomous ML Engineering?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.40303)
*Kirill Brilliantov,Alejandro Hernández-Cano,Emmanuel Abbé*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究了现代MLE（机器学习工程）代理中复杂框架的必要性。研究发现在相同时间预算和相同前沿LLM骨干下，复杂的开源MLE框架相比最小框架的编码代理基线没有优势，性能的主要驱动因素是骨干模型本身。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代MLE代理部署了越来越复杂的机制，包括多代理编排器、专用检索子代理等，但作者注意到这些复杂框架与使用更原始但改进的编码代理（LLM可直接通过读写和bash原语访问执行环境）之间的对比缺乏关注。

Method: 在相同时间预算和相同前沿LLM骨干下，进行了一系列大规模系统性消融实验，比较了最先进的开源框架与最小框架编码代理基线的性能。

Result: 研究发现，开源的最先进框架相比最小框架的编码代理基线没有提供任何优势，这表明骨干模型是性能的主要驱动因素，框架层在编码代理设置中变得冗余。

Conclusion: 在当前MLE基准测试中，围绕强模型精心设计复杂框架的努力收效甚微，应该更加关注提升骨干模型本身的能力。

Abstract: Recent autonomous machine learning engineering (MLE) agents have made significant progress on public leaderboards. Often motivated by progress stagnation over long-horizon cycles and limited Large Language Model (LLM) primitives, modern MLE agents are deployed on top of increasingly elaborate machinery: multi-agent orchestrators, dedicated retrieval subagents, and more. While such harnesses expand, the use of more primitive but improved coding agents - where LLMs have direct access to the execution environment through read, write, and bash primitives - has received little attention in the field. In this paper we find that, under an equal time budget and the same frontier LLM backbone, open-source state-of-the-art harnesses provide no advantages over a single session of a minimal-harness coding agent baseline, pointing to the backbone as the primary driver for performance. Via a series of large-scale systematic ablation studies, we argue that the machinery layers become redundant in the coding agent setting. We conclude that the effort spent elaborating hand-crafted harnesses around strong models yields poor returns for current MLE benchmarks.

</details>


### [328] [Cogentic: Multi-Agent Orchestration for Automated Proof Discovery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.40324)
*Yang Cai,Vineet Gupta,Yanchen Jiang,Christopher Liaw,Aranyak Mehta,Grigoris Velegkas,Di Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Summary generation failed


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Motivation analysis unavailable

Method: Method extraction failed

Result: Result analysis unavailable

Conclusion: Conclusion extraction failed

Abstract: We present Cogentic, a multi-agent harness for automated proof discovery on open research problems. While frontier language models can generate strong mathematical ideas in a single shot, single-shot generation is often insufficient for open problems that require exploring multiple competing conjectures, overcoming subtle technical obstructions, and retaining intermediate progress over a long horizon. Cogentic addresses these challenges through an iterative prove--verify loop in which an orchestrator allocates a population of independent provers across distinct proof directions, subjects their output to adversarial verification by several specialized components, and promotes confirmed intermediate results into a persistent verified ledger that later rounds build on. The harness is designed to be able to solve research-level math and theoretical computer science problems. Using Gemini as the base model, Cogentic produced novel results on five open problems across online learning, auction theory, and mechanism design. Each result was independently verified by domain experts and is developed in full in companion papers. We list these results, and new ones as they are verified, at https://sites.google.com/view/cogentic .

</details>


### [329] [WorldAuditBench: Interactive 3D World Auditing with Multimodal Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.40325)
*Ziyan Jiang,Jingbo Yang,Jiabao Ji,Yujian Liu,Qiucheng Wu,Tommi Jaakkola,Yang Zhang,Shiyu Chang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: WorldAuditBench是一个用于审计3D模拟世界的基准测试，旨在识别异常目标如漂浮物体、可穿越墙壁等。该基准包含213个异常任务，涵盖13个环境和5个异常家族。评估结果显示，当前前沿多模态模型的成功率仅为6.6%-42.3%，远低于人类表现(83.4%)。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着交互式3D世界越来越多地被用于研究智能行为，开发有效的异常识别管道变得重要。3D世界审计具有复杂性，需要将动作(导航和搜索)与视觉推理(理解和识别异常)紧密结合。研究多模态智能体是否能有效结合这两种能力具有重要意义。

Method: 构建WorldAuditBench基准，包含213个异常任务，涵盖13个使用Unreal Engine 5和Three.js构建的环境，分为5个异常家族。在固定探索预算下评估5个前沿模型，采用两种审计范式：1)基于VLA的探索+基于VLM的异常识别；2)端到端VLM智能体，视觉推理直接指导动作选择。

Result: 在评估的模型和两种范式中，成功率范围为6.6%至42.3%，显著低于人类表现(83.4%)。

Conclusion: WorldAuditBench为研究多模态智能体如何在交互式3D环境中耦合动作与视觉推理提供了测试平台，同时揭示了当前模型在探索过程中收集和解释证据能力的局限性。

Abstract: As interactive 3D worlds are increasingly used to study intelligent behavior, it becomes important to develop efficient pipelines for identifying anomalies in these simulated environments, such as floating objects, traversable walls, or objects inconsistent with the surrounding scene. Multimodal AI systems, including vision-language models (VLMs) and vision-language-action models (VLAs), have shown potential for automating this task. However, 3D world auditing is complex, requiring the close coupling of two distinct capabilities: action, to navigate the 3D world and search for anomalies systematically and efficiently; and visual reasoning, to understand the environment and identify anomalies from multimodal observations. It remains largely unexplored whether multimodal agents can effectively couple these two capabilities, using visual reasoning to identify potential anomalies while taking actions to validate them. In this paper, we introduce WorldAuditBench, a benchmark for 3D world auditing comprising 213 anomaly tasks across 13 environments built with Unreal Engine 5 and Three.js, spanning five anomaly families. We evaluate five frontier models under a fixed exploration budget using two auditing paradigms: VLA-based exploration followed by VLM-based anomaly identification, and an end-to-end VLM agent in which visual reasoning directly guides action selection. Across the evaluated models and two paradigms, success rates range from 6.6% to 42.3%, substantially below human performance (83.4%). Through the task of world auditing, WorldAuditBench provides a testbed for studying how multimodal agents couple action and visual reasoning in interactive 3D environments, while highlighting current limitations in their ability to gather and interpret evidence during exploration.

</details>


### [330] [Turbo Harness: Instance-Adaptive Harness Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.40330)
*Tunyu Zhang,Hao Wang,Kai Xu,Dimitris N. Metaxas*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Turbo Harness是一个框架，通过重用全局harness优化过程中生成的信息，将全局优化的harness适应到每个任务实例。具体做法是将优化过程中的产物回收整理成结构化的"playbook"，训练一个harness编辑器利用这些先验优化经验为每个实例生成特定的补丁。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的harness优化通常产生一个全局适用的harness，但平均最优的harness不一定在每个实例上都表现最佳。需要一种方法能够根据每个实例的特点定制harness。

Method: Turbo Harness回收已完成全局harness优化运行的产物，将其总结为结构化的playbook。训练一个harness编辑器利用这些先验优化经验，在推理时根据实例和playbook生成针对该实例定制的harness补丁。

Result: 通过在七个基准测试上的数值实验表明，Turbo Harness始终优于现有的harness优化基线方法。这些基准涵盖了交互式agent任务、软件工程和长视界终端任务。

Conclusion: Turbo Harness能够有效地将全局优化经验迁移到实例级别的harness定制中，在多种任务类型上取得了一致的性能提升。

Abstract: Automating the search for effective harnesses is an important step toward enabling agents to recursively self-improve. Existing harness optimizations typically produce a single global harness that is applied uniformly across task instances. However, a harness that works well on average may not be optimal for every instance. We introduce Turbo Harness, a framework that can adapt a globally optimized harness to each instance by reusing information generated during the original optimization process. Specifically, Turbo Harness recycles artifacts produced during a completed global harness optimization run, and summarizes them into a structured playbook. We train a harness editor to leverage this prior optimization experience to generate instance-specific patches to the global harness. At inference time, the editor uses the instance and the playbook to construct a tailored harness in which the execution model operates. Through numerical experiments, we show that Turbo Harness consistently outperforms existing harness optimization baselines across seven benchmarks spanning interactive agent tasks, software engineering, and long-horizon terminal tasks.

</details>


<div id='cs.CG'></div>

# cs.CG [[Back]](#toc)

### [331] [Polynomial-time algorithm for exact $(1,2)$-center problem under continuous Fréchet distance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39292)
*Soumya Bhattacharya,Serene Rasheed,Sasanka Roy*

Main category: cs.CG

TL;DR: 本文研究了连续Fréchet距离下多边形的(1,2)-中心问题，提出了多项式时间精确算法和近似算法。对于平面曲线，运行时间为O((n²r+nr²)^{{2+ε}})，对于d维空间为O((n²r+nr²)^{{2(d-1)+ε}})。还给出了(1+ε)因子近似算法和3因子近似算法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究动机：(1,2)-中心问题是经典的(k,ℓ)-中心问题的特例，已知是NP-hard的。之前Aronov等人(WADS'19)给出了离散Fréchet距离下的多项式时间算法，但连续Fréchet距离下的(1,2)中心问题尚未被研究。该问题在计算几何、模式识别等领域有重要应用。

Method: 研究方法：1) 基于de Berg等人(CCCG'17)提出的曲线与查询段之间Fréchet距离的公式，证明了该公式在L₂和L∞范数下对任意R^d曲线都有效；2) 采用几何算法设计技术，包括区域划分和动态规划；3) 对于近似算法，采用ε-近似技术。

Result: 研究成果：1) 平面曲线在L₂和L范数下的精确算法时间为O((n²r+nr²)^{{2+ε}})；2) d维曲线期望时间为O((n²r+nr²)^{{2(d-1)+ε}})；3) (1+ε)因子近似算法时间为O(n²r+nr²+1/ε^s)；4) 限制水平中心时精确时间为O(n²r+nr²)；5) 3因子近似算法时间为输入曲线数线性时间。

Conclusion: 结论：本文首次解决了连续Fréchet距离下的(1,2)-中心问题，给出了精确多项式时间算法和近似算法。证明了该问题在连续Fréchet距离下是可解的，为计算几何中曲线中心化问题提供了新的算法框架。

Abstract: In this paper, we explore the $(1,2)$-center problem for polygonal curves under continuous Fréchet distance. The $(k,\ell)$-center problem, in general, is known to be NP-hard. Aronov, Filtser, Horton, Katz, and Sheikhan (WADS'19) gave a polynomial-time algorithm for the $(1,2)$-center of curves in the plane under the discrete Fréchet distance. To the best of our knowledge, the $(1,2)$-center under continuous Fréchet distance has not been studied yet. We present a polynomial time algorithm to solve the problem exactly under both $\mathbb{{L}}_2$ and $\mathbb{{L}}_\infty$ norm, running in $O\bigr((n^2r+nr^2)^{{2+ε}}\bigl)$ time for curves in the plane where $r$ is the number of input curves and $n$ is the maximum complexity of any curve. Further, for curves in any dimension $d$, the expected time to compute the center using the algorithm is $O\bigr((n^2r+nr^2)^{{2(d-1)+ε}}\bigl)$. We have also shown that an $(1+\widetildeε)-$factor approximation of $(1,2)$-center can be computed in $O(n^2r+nr^2+1/ε^s)$ time for any $ε>\widetildeε>0$ and some constant $s$ for curves in the plane. For curves in the plane, we have shown that, with the center restricted to be horizontal, we can compute the exact center in $O(n^2r+nr^2)$ time. An algorithm has been introduced to find a $3$-factor approximation of the $(1,2)$-center in time linear in the number of curves. A formulation was introduced by de Berg, Mehrabi, and Ophelders (CCCG'17) to measure Fréchet distance between a curve and a query segment under $\mathbb{{L}}_2$ norm for curves in the plane. We have shown the formulation is valid under both $\mathbb{{L}}_2$ and $\mathbb{{L}}_\infty$ norm for curves in $\mathbb{{R}}^d$.

</details>


### [332] [A Unified Dual Method for Matching Problems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39339)
*Guillaume Houry,Ferdinand Genans,Jean Feydy,François-Xavier Vialard*

Main category: cs.CG

TL;DR: 该论文将一大类匹配问题统一在基于对偶理论的数学优化框架中，展示了可分解为凸函数差（DC）的匹配目标可以重新表述为隐式配准问题，并应用于二次匹配（QM）问题，为Gromov-Wasserstein及其不平衡变体提供收敛保证，实现大规模算法实现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的求解器通常针对特定的匹配公式量身定制，缺乏一个统一的数学和优化框架来涵盖广泛类型的匹配问题。

Method: 基于对偶理论，将匹配目标（可分解为凸函数差（DC））重新表述为隐式配准问题，得到可采用自然优化策略的对偶公式。应用于二次匹配（QM）问题。

Result: 框架适用于Gromov-Wasserstein（GW）及其不平衡形式和多种变体。在图、点云、网格和词嵌入等多种数据模态上实现了大规模算法实现。还探索了新的 fracture matching 公式。

Conclusion: 提供了一个统一的模块化框架，能够处理广泛匹配问题，包括新的 fracture matching，扩展了可解决的匹配问题范围。

Abstract: Matching problems are ubiquitous in data science as they enable the alignment of structured objects and distributions. While existing solvers are often tailored to specific matching formulations, we unify a broad class of such problems within a common mathematical and optimization framework based on duality theory. Theoretically, we demonstrate that matching objectives decomposable as a difference of convex (DC) functions can be recast as implicit registration problems. This connection links matching to another well-studied class of objectives and yields a dual formulation amenable to natural optimization strategies. We then apply these findings to quadratic matching (QM) problems, which admit DC decompositions and for which we provide extensive convergence guarantees. Our framework applies to Gromov-Wasserstein (GW), as well as its unbalanced formulation and several variants, which are increasingly popular QM problems. Numerically, we implement our algorithms at scale for various data modalities such as graphs, point clouds, meshes, and word embeddings. Finally, our modular approach allows us to explore new formulations such as fracture matching, broadening the scope of problems that can be addressed within this framework..

</details>


### [333] [Flexible discrete translational surfaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39517)
*Georg Nawratil*

Main category: cs.CG

TL;DR: 本文解决了在其对应的离散平移曲面类中可弯曲的平移网格的完整分类问题，通过将分类问题归约为球面上完全二分框架的灵活构型问题，并基于 Bottema 球面 16 杆机构和常对角角框架获得了两个新的类别。此外，还讨论了灵活平移管和环面体，并提出了由 Bottema 球面 16 杆机构暗示的关于这两种拓扑的新结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 平移网格是离散平移曲面中的重要研究对象，而其灵活性的分类问题一直是该领域的核心难题。之前的研究缺乏系统性的分类方法，尤其是对于具有特殊拓扑结构的平移网格。

Method: 通过将平移网格的分类问题归约为球面上完全二分框架的灵活构型问题，利用已知的球面框架分类结果来推导平移网格的分类。同时开发了构建所有灵活平移网格的算法。

Result: 获得了平移网格的完整分类列表，发现了两个新类别：对应于 Bottema 球面 16 杆机构的类别和常对角角框架类别。此外，还讨论了灵活平移管和环面体，并获得了由 Bottema 球面 16 杆机构暗示的新结果。

Conclusion: 该研究为平移网格的灵活性提供了完整的分类理论，为进一步研究离散曲面和空间机构的运动特性奠定了基础，同时也为机械设计和几何建模提供了新的理论工具。

Abstract: We give a full list of translational nets which flex within their class of discrete surfaces of translation, by reducing the classification problem to the one of flexible complete bipartite frameworks on the sphere, for which the solution is known. We also obtained two novel classes which correspond to Bottema's spherical 16-bar mechanisms and the constant diagonal angle frameworks. Based on an algorithm for the construction of all flexible translational nets, we also discuss flexible translational tubes and toroids. Furthermore, we present novel results for both topologies which are implied by Bottema's spherical 16-bar mechanisms.

</details>


### [334] [Parameterized Hardness of Mixed 2-Sided Orthant Depth](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39718)
*Michelle Döring,Georg Tennigkeit*

Main category: cs.CG

TL;DR: 本文研究了R^d中混合双边象限的最大深度问题，证明了该问题的决策问题在以维度为参数时是W[1]困难的。该结果基于从MultiColoredClique的归约，使用每个颜色类两个坐标和多象限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究混合2边象限的最大深度问题的计算复杂性，旨在理解该几何优化问题在参数化复杂性理论中的难度定位，特别是在以空间维度作为参数的情况下。

Method: 采用参数化复杂性理论的方法，从MultiColoredClique问题进行归约，每个颜色类使用两个坐标，并使用多项式数量的象限来构造实例。

Result: 证明了最大深度问题对于混合2边象限，在以维度d为参数时是W[1]困难的，这表明该问题不太可能存在固定参数 tractable (FPT)算法。

Conclusion: 该结果揭示了即使在相对简单的几何区域（混合2边象限）上，最大深度问题在参数化复杂性框架下也是困难的，为理解几何优化问题的固有复杂性提供了重要贡献。

Abstract: We consider the maximum-depth problem for mixed 2-sided orthants in R^d: each region imposes one lower bound and one upper bound on distinct coordinates, and the task is to find a point contained in as many regions as possible. We show that the corresponding decision problem is W[1]-hard when parameterized by the dimension. Our reduction from MultiColoredClique uses two coordinates per color class and only polynomially many orthants.

</details>
